### [73] [Beyond Rough Volatility: Decoupling Memory and Scaling via a Generalized Langevin Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20293)
*Andrey Itkin*

Main category: q-fin.MF

TL;DR: 本文将非平衡统计力学中的广义朗之万方程(GLE)引入随机波动率建模，以克服分数布朗运动(fBm)的结构性局限，将"波动率标度"与"记忆性"解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fBm作为粗糙波动率的标准引擎，强迫单一参数同时决定两个逻辑独立的属性：波动率如何标度、如何记忆。这一结构性限制需要新的框架来分离这两者。

Method: 引入广义朗之万方程(GLE)作为随机波动率框架，使用记忆核K、势函数U和噪声协方差C三个组件，使记忆成为可测量的对象；非对称势提供了对方差偏斜的控制杠杆。

Result: 在公开数据集上的物理测度检验明确拒绝了两类受限情形——无记忆杠杆效应和时间反演对称性；而核心的粗糙标度约束则因识别性问题受限，未被证伪。作者也坦诚报告了这些局限。

Conclusion: GLE成功解耦标度与记忆，并提供了fBm所不具备的方差偏斜调控能力；但粗糙标度约束的识别性、工业级数据验证仍是待解决问题，风险中性构造与SPX-VIX联合校准将在后续论文中展开。

Abstract: Borrowed from non-equilibrium statistical mechanics, the generalized Langevin equation (GLE) is imported as a framework for stochastic volatility to address the structural limitations of fractional Brownian motion (fBm), the standard engine of rough volatility. The fBm forces a single parameter to set two logically independent properties at once: how volatility scales and how it remembers. The GLE separates them using a memory kernel $K$, a potential $U$, and a noise covariance $C$. Memory becomes a measurable object, and an asymmetric potential supplies a lever on variance skew that the price-variance correlation cannot reach. Physical-measure tests on public datasets decisively reject two constrained corners of the class, a memoryless leverage effect and time-reversal symmetry, while the central rough scaling constraint is left identification-limited rather than refuted. The paper reports these limits honestly, and validation on industry-grade data remains a valuable direction. The risk-neutral construction and the joint SPX--VIX calibration will be developed in a companion paper.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [74] [Persistent mixed worldviews in the theory of chosen preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19316)
*Ziran Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文修正了Bernheim等(2021, AER)"选择偏好"理论中关于低思维灵活性下收敛到纯世界观的猜想，证明该猜想在多种世界观下不成立，并给出持续性条件与收敛到纯性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bernheim等(2021)提出选择偏好理论并猜想低思维灵活性下会收敛到纯世界观。作者发现原猜想有误，需要重新刻画混合世界观何时持续、何时收敛到纯性。

Method: 用有限线性规划刻画混合世界观的持续性充分条件；用共享估值最大值条件分析混合聚点；在可实施动作具唯一最大化评估者等假设下分析收敛性，并将结果推广到平稳随机化。

Result: 低思维灵活性下原猜想失败：存在相对开集的初始世界观使混合世界观在每个平稳均衡中持续，即使所有纯世界观均吸收。有限线性规划给出持续性充分条件；混合聚点要求共享估值最大值。若每个可实施动作有唯一最大化评估者，则对所有内部灵活性与贴现参数，所有平稳均衡收敛到纯性。经验效用无需唯一性即收敛。平稳均衡下小收益扰动以高概率在固定期限保持妥协。严格定制世界观在足够低灵活性下给出存在性与一步均衡刻画；弱实施可能排除平稳纯策略存在。

Conclusion: 修正并深化了选择偏好理论：混合世界观可持续，收敛到纯性需额外条件，为理解世界观动态提供新工具与结论。

Abstract: Bernheim, Braghieri, Martínez-Marquina, and Zuckerman (2021, American Economic Review) develop a theory of chosen preferences and conjecture convergence to pure worldviews at low mindset flexibility. We show that the (original) conjecture fails: with arbitrarily many worldviews, a common-value action sustains mixed worldviews in every stationary equilibrium from a relatively open set of initial worldviews, even when all pure worldviews are absorbing.
  However, finite linear programs characterize a sufficient persistence condition. Conversely, mixed accumulation points require shared valuation maxima. If each implementable action has a unique maximizing evaluator, every existing stationary equilibrium converges to purity for all interior flexibility and discount parameters. Experienced utility converges without uniqueness. In existing equilibria, small payoff perturbations preserve compromise over fixed horizons with high probability. Strictly tailored worldviews yield existence and a one-step equilibrium characterization at sufficiently low mindset flexibility; weak implementation can preclude stationary pure-strategy existence. The persistence and convergence results extend to stationary randomization.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出NomadBBO，一个面向混合变量黑箱优化的Python库，核心是扩展ADS的CatADS直接搜索方法，结合类别变量邻域与渐进障碍处理约束，在Cat-Suite基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合变量黑箱优化在仿真工程与机器学习中广泛存在，目标函数与约束评估昂贵，变量可为连续、整数、二元或分类。现有无导数优化库虽多，但很少同时兼顾易用性、计算效率、灵活性与理论收敛保证。

Method: 提出NomadBBO库及核心方法CatADS：将ADS的定量变量机制与分类变量邻域相结合，邻域采用基于高斯过程或经验Wasserstein的距离；用渐进障碍处理不等式约束；混合变量与代理机制用Python实现，经Cython连接Nomad的C++后端，从而支持分类变量并可集成SMT 2.0高斯过程与贝叶斯优化。

Result: 在Cat-Suite基准的约束与非约束混合变量问题上，将NomadBBO与其他求解器进行了数值对比实验。Beta版本已在测试PyPI发布。

Conclusion: NomadBBO实现了易用且理论有保证的混合变量黑箱优化，并通过软硬件集成支持外部库与混合优化策略。

Abstract: Mixed-variable blackbox optimization arises in simulation-based engineering and machine learning, where objective and constraint functions are expensive to evaluate and variables may be continuous, integer, binary, or categorical. Several software libraries are available for derivative-free optimization, but few combine ease of use, computational efficiency, flexibility, and theoretical convergence guarantees. This work presents NomadBBO, a user-friendly Python library for inequality-constrained mixed-variable blackbox optimization built around Nomad. Its core optimization framework is CatADS, a new direct-search method that extends Adaptive Direct Search (ADS) to mixed-variable problems. CatADS combines the mechanisms of ADS for quantitative variables with neighborhoods for handling categorical variables. These neighborhoods use Gaussian process-based or empirical Wasserstein distances derived from available data. Inequality constraints are handled through the progressive barrier. The mixed-variable and surrogate-based mechanisms are implemented in Python and connected to the efficient C++ backend of Nomad via Cython. This integration allows Nomad to handle categorical variables. It also enables the use of external libraries and hybrid optimization strategies, including Gaussian process models from SMT 2.0 and Bayesian optimization. Numerical experiments compare NomadBBO with other solvers on constrained and unconstrained mixed-variable problems from the Cat-Suite benchmark collection. The beta release is available at https://test.pypi.org/project/NomadBBO/

</details>


### [76] [Computing the convex envelope of bivariate piecewise linear-quadratic functions in linear time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19343)
*Tanmaya Karmarkar,Yves Lucet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出计算二元分段线性二次(PLQ)函数凸包的五步算法，可在近线性时间内得到凸包，并证明其为分段有理函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文关注非凸二元分段线性二次(PLQ)函数的凸包计算问题，旨在给出一个实用且高效的算法，并阐明凸包函数的结构形式。

Method: 算法分五步：1) 计算每个二次块的凸包，得到多面体剖分上的分段有理函数（二次除以线性）；2) 对每块求Legendre-Fenchel共轭，得到抛物剖分上的分段二次函数；3) 取最大值得到原PLQ函数的共轭，为抛物剖分上的分段二次函数；4) 对每块再求共轭；5) 取最大值得到凸包（双共轭）为多面体剖分上的有理函数。

Result: 给出了一个实际可运行的线性时间算法；并证明该凸包为分段有理函数（二次除以线性），定义在多面体剖分上。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种高效计算二元PLQ函数凸包的方法，并证明了凸包的分段有理结构。

Abstract: We compute the convex envelope of (nonconvex) bivariate piecewise linear-quadratic (PLQ) functions (bivariate quadratic functions defined on a polyhedral subdivision). Our algorithm is composed of the following steps: (1) compute the convex envelope of each quadratic piece obtaining piecewise rational functions (quadratic divided by linear function) defined over a polyhedral subdivision; (2) compute the (Legendre-Fenchel) conjugate of each resulting piece to obtain piecewise quadratic functions defined over a parabolic subdivision; (3) compute the maximum of all those functions to obtain the conjugate of the original PLQ function as a piecewise quadratic function defined on a parabolic subdivision; (4) compute the conjugate of each resulting piece; and finally (5) compute the maximum over all those functions to obtain the convex envelope (biconjugate) as rational functions (quadratic divided by linear function) defined over a polyhedral subdivision. Our contribution includes a practical algorithm running in linear time, and proving that the convex envelope is a piecewise rational function.

</details>


### [77] [Where the LLM Ends and Reliable Decisions Begin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19545)
*Priyadarshan Patil,Abhishek Basu,Vikas Reddy,Abhinav Gupta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: ANVIL 将自然语言优化问题转代码的流程拆分为：LLM 只调用一次生成 LaTeX 公式，再由确定性编译器将其翻译成可执行代码，在 NLP4LP 上达到 92.9% 编译率与高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统在包括最终数学公式到代码翻译的每一阶段都使用语言模型，导致不必要且不可靠的模型调用。作者认为应仅在 LLM 真正擅长的环节使用它。

Method: 提出 ANVIL 编译器架构：LLM 仅调用一次，在问题类型约束引导下生成 LaTeX 公式；随后由确定性编译器（归一化器、递归下降解析器生成带类型中间表示、符号绑定到数据集模式的分析流程、代码发射器）翻译为代码，全程无模型参与。

Result: 在 NLP4LP 的 354 道题（简单+困难）上，编译器为 329 道（92.9%）生成代码，全部走确定性路径、从未回退到模型生成；中位编译时间低于计时器 10ms 分辨率。准确率：简单题 98.9%，困难题 91.1%。

Conclusion: 编译率与准确率之间的差距主要源于公式本身而非翻译过程。ANVIL 只在语言模型有效之处使用它，从而在领先基准上表现优异。

Abstract: Systems that turn natural-language descriptions of optimization problems into solver-ready code generally use a language model at every stage, including the final translation from a mathematical formulation into executable model-building code. We propose the ANVIL compiler architecture, where we separate these concerns. A language model is called only once, assisted by constraint guidance based on problem type, to produce a LaTeX formulation. A deterministic compiler then translates that LaTeX into code with no language model involvement. We describe the deterministic compiler (a normalizer, a recursive-descent parser producing a typed intermediate representation, analysis passes that bind symbols to a dataset schema, and a code emitter) and evaluate it on the 354 easy and hard problems of the NLP4LP benchmark. The compiler produced code for 329 of 354 formulations (92.9%), taking the deterministic path in every one of those cases and never falling back to model-generated code. Median compile time was below the 10ms resolution of our timer. Overall, our formulations achieved an accuracy of 98.9% over easy problems and 91.1% for hard problems. The gap between these compilation and accuracy figures is a key point of analysis, and we analyze it: formulations that failed to compile, problems that returned as infeasible, problems raising errors at runtime, and problems returning a wrong objective. Almost all of these errors trace back to the formulation rather than to the translation. ANVIL performs exceptionally well on leading benchmarks by using language models purely where they are effective, rather than as a catch-all tool.

</details>


### [78] [PDNQP: A GPU-based Factorization-free Method for Large-scale Nonconvex Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19557)
*Zixi Chen,Haihao Lu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: PDNQP是首个无分解的一阶求解器，用于大规模非凸二次规划求驻点；结合近端增广拉格朗日与重启加速原始-对偶混合梯度，在CUTEst和百万变量合成QP上达到最高成功率与显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有求解器依赖稀疏矩阵分解，计算与内存开销大、难以在GPU上高效并行，导致大规模非凸QP求解困难。

Method: 提出PDNQP：近端增广拉格朗日框架 + 重启加速原始-对偶混合梯度求解强凸QP子问题；采用残差形式重构避免惩罚相关正规矩阵并保持固定稀疏约束算子，并用自适应策略协调内层精度与外层进度；计算仅依赖稀疏矩阵-向量乘法、投影和向量运算，适合GPU。

Result: 107个非凸CUTEst实例上成功率最高；容差10^{-4}时加速2.6×–6.0×，10^{-6}时总运行时间与最快求解器相当；30个约百万变量的合成非凸QP中27个满足外部终止准则，其他求解器均无成功。

Conclusion: PDNQP为大规模非凸QP提供了一种可扩展、GPU友好的无分解一阶方法，在标准与大规模测试中均优于现有求解器。

Abstract: Large-scale nonconvex quadratic programming remains challenging because the sparse matrix factorizations used by many existing solvers can incur substantial computational and memory costs and are difficult to parallelize efficiently on GPUs. We present PDNQP, a factorization-free first-order solver for finding stationary points of large-scale nonconvex quadratic programs. The method combines a proximal augmented-Lagrangian framework with restarted accelerated primal--dual hybrid gradient to solve the resulting strongly convex QP subproblems. Its key ingredients are a residual-form reformulation that avoids penalty-dependent normal matrices and preserves a fixed sparse constraint operator across outer iterations, and an adaptive strategy that coordinates inner-solve accuracy with outer progress. The resulting computations rely on sparse matrix--vector products, projections, and vector operations suited to GPU execution. On $107$ nonconvex CUTEst instances, PDNQP achieves the highest success rate among the tested solvers at both accuracy levels. With absolute and relative tolerances set to $10^{-4}$, it achieves speedups of $2.6\times$--$6.0\times$ in shifted geometric mean runtime; at $10^{-6}$, its aggregate runtime remains comparable to that of the fastest tested solvers. On $30$ large-scale synthetic nonconvex QPs with approximately one million variables each, PDNQP returns points satisfying the common external termination criteria on $27$ instances, while none of the other tested solvers succeeds under the same experimental protocol.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疫情缓解措施存在成本，预算有限时如何有效分配资源成为重要公共政策问题，需要刻画政府与大量理性个体的策略互动。

Method: 构建Stackelberg平均场博弈：政府（主方）在预算支出过程约束下制定社交距离与疫苗接种政策，预期民众的平均场纳什均衡（MFNE）以最小化自身目标，形成双层最优控制问题；给出Stackelberg平均场博弈均衡（SMFE）定义及其由前向-后向常微分方程（FBODE）系统表示的均衡必要条件，并给出存在唯一性结果；提出迭代不动点算法数值求解FBODE。

Result: 给出SMFE的FBODE必要条件及存在唯一性，提出迭代不动点算法实现数值求解，并通过计算观察展示均衡行为。

Conclusion: 该框架为有限预算下疫情防控政策的有效分配提供了理论刻画与数值求解工具，能够揭示政府干预与民众响应的均衡行为。

Abstract: Epidemic mitigation comes with a price in practice, and when budget is limited, its effective allocation becomes an important question on public policy. In this paper, we study epidemic control through a Stackelberg mean-field game in which a principal, representing the government, chooses social distancing guidelines and vaccination levels for a large population of rational minor agents subject to a budget-spending process. The principal anticipates the minor agents mean-field Nash equilibrium (MFNE) and selects intervention policies to minimize her own objective, inducing a bi-level optimal control problem. We define the Stackelberg mean-field game equilibrium (SMFE) and give its necessary condition by a forward-backward ordinary differential equation (FBODE) system, and provide existence and uniqueness results. We further propose an iterative fixed-point algorithm to solve the FBODE numerically and present computational observations that illustrate the resulting equilibrium behavior.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 给出跟随者在给定领导者努力下的最优策略；刻画领导者最大化期望效用的最优策略；计算双方在均衡策略下的期望效用，并揭示玩家能力异质性与失败成本如何影响博弈结果。

Conclusion: 该模型为理解带有努力失败风险和玩家异质性的序贯竞赛提供了策略刻画与均衡分析，说明能力差异和失败成本是决定竞赛结果的重要因素。

Abstract: We study a Stackelberg model where two players compete in a sequential contest, and the player that successfully invests the most effort wins. Our formulation considers a probability that each player fails to invest their chosen level of effort, as captured by a failure function that increases with the amount of effort. The players each have their own cost of failure, as well as a maximum level of effort (capturing different abilities of the players). We prove three key results. First, we provide the optimal strategy for Player 1 (the follower), to maximize their utility given an observed effort exerted by Player 2 (the leader). Next, we characterize the optimal strategy to maximize the expected utility of Player 2, anticipating the optimal response by Player 1. Finally, we analyze the expected utilities of each player when both play their optimal strategy, and provide key insights into how heterogeneous player abilities and failure costs affect the outcomes of the game.

</details>

Abstract: We study the convergence of the vanilla stochastic policy gradient method applied to the linear quadratic regulator (LQR) problem. The method is cheap in the following sense: (1) at each iteration only $\tilde{O}(1)$ interactions with the environment are needed, therefore allowing frequent policy improvement steps, and (2) to ensure stability throughout and convergence to an $ε$-optimal policy with probability $1-δ$, only $O(\mathtt{Polylog}(1/δ)/ε)$ interactions are needed. To the best of our knowledge, this appears to be the first time that a stochastic model-free policy optimization method for LQR converges with high probability with $\tilde{O}(1)$ per-iteration computation and polylogarithmic dependence on the confidence level. The convergence analysis presented here is agnostic to LQR specifics and hence could be potentially generalized to a broader class of problems.

Abstract: The research studies the extended mean-field anticipated forward-backward stochastic delayed differential equations with common noise (extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN) by expanding the domination-monotonicity conditions. We generalize the conditions from a linear setting to a nonlinear one by including nonlinear adjoint functions which are key to guaranteeing the well-posedness of extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN. Utilizing this broader well-posedness framework for extended MF-AFBSDDEswCN, combined with other refined analytical tools, we examine two classes of stochastic optimal control problems. These include a linear-convex problem and a linear-quadratic problem with input constraints that can be both time-dependent and random. Moreover, the control elements regarding the initial values are path-dependent, which will cause some essential difficulties. Conclusion: 最优传输为成对比较理论提供了一致的统一视角，所提方法在理论与数值上均得到验证，并能与文献中已有方法进行有效对比。

Abstract: In this paper, we reinterpret pairwise comparison theory through the lens of optimal transport. The main contribution is the connection established between pairwise comparison matrices, doubly stochastic scaling, and entropy-regularized transport problems. Using Sinkhorn scaling, we show how consistency, priority derivation, and inconsistency assessment can be studied within a common optimal-transport framework. As methodological consequences of this connection, we obtain a new characterization of consistency, a Sinkhorn-based prioritization method, and an entropy-based inconsistency index. We study these proposals both analytically and numerically and compare them with established methods from the literature

Abstract: We study Percolation Markov Decision Processes (PMDPs), in which the decision maker repeatedly moves a token through the d-dimensional integer lattice with deterministic transitions and random payoffs assigned to the edges. Payoffs are revealed before the beginning of the decision process and the decision maker aims to maximize the average accumulated payoff over a fixed horizon. We establish the existence of the uniform value (as the horizon tends to infinity) and 0-optimal strategies. In the particular case of Bernoulli payoffs, we establish continuity results for the uniform value. We also present several one-dimensional examples to illustrate that optimal strategies may be very complex. Then, we introduce a dimensional lifting property that allows PMDPs to be approximated by PMDPs of lower dimension and could be used to approximate critical probability thresholds in percolation theory.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce a new model for a regulated day-ahead type auction-based electrical market in which the system operator can measure and limit the producer's emissions when choosing its optimal dispatch.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三维旋转群（SO(3)）上、无向树图拓扑下研究分布式优化，智能体具有异构局部代价，需在测地凸球内寻找使测地强凸代价和最小的公共姿态。测地强凸性本身不足以保证不变性（论文给出反例），因此需要额外条件与协议设计。

Method: 提出固定增益非光滑协议：局部黎曼梯度下降 + 基于相邻旋转Lie群对数（log）的相对旋转符号（signum）一致性反馈。引入内指向边界条件保证球内不变性。构造非光滑本征不一致性Lyapunov函数，利用树结构的簇收缩论证得到一致耗散界。

Result: 在局部梯度有界下有显式增益比条件，可对所有Filippov解建立有限时间精确一致性，并给出仅依赖智能体数量、无需图结构常数的收敛时间估计。一致后滑动动力学为求和目标的缩放黎曼梯度流；当最优解位于球内部时，按测地距离指数收敛。

Conclusion: 在SO(3)树图上实现有限时间分布式共识与优化，给出不含图结构常数的收敛保证，仿真验证了理论结果。

Abstract: We study continuous-time distributed optimization on the three-dimensional rotation group over undirected tree graphs, where agents with heterogeneous local costs seek a common attitude minimizing the geodesically strongly convex sum of their costs on a geodesically convex operating ball. We propose a nonsmooth protocol with fixed gains that combines local Riemannian gradient descent with signum-based consensus feedback computed from the Lie-group logarithms of relative rotations between neighboring agents. Since geodesic strong convexity alone does not guarantee invariance, as demonstrated by a certified counterexample, we impose an inward-pointing boundary condition and prove that every Filippov solution with all agents initialized in the ball remains there. We construct a nonsmooth intrinsic disagreement Lyapunov function and derive a uniform dissipation bound through a cluster-contraction argument exploiting the tree structure. With bounded local gradients, this bound gives an explicit gain-ratio condition under which we establish exact finite-time consensus for every such solution and obtain a settling-time estimate requiring no structural graph constant beyond the number of agents. After consensus, we identify the sliding dynamics as a scaled Riemannian gradient flow of the summed objective. When the minimizer lies in the interior of the ball, the dynamics converge to it exponentially in geodesic distance. Simulations illustrate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [87] [Optimal Design of Distributed Inexact Gradient Tracking: Exact Worst-case Convergence Rate and Explicit Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20265)
*Qiuchen Tian,Li Chai,Jinming Xu*

TL;DR: 本文提出一个确定分布式优化算法精确最坏情况收敛速率的框架，针对DIGing算法，通过图信号处理与鲁棒控制理论给出显式公式，并数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式优化方法通常仅给出充分条件和保守的收敛速率，缺乏对精确最坏情况收敛速率的刻画。

Method: 在μ-强凸、L-Lipschitz目标函数及给定代数连通度的连通图集合上定义最坏情况收敛；聚焦DIGing算法，结合图信号处理与鲁棒控制理论，提出新的分解结构实现部分子系统解耦，推导精确最坏情况收敛速率及对应参数的显式公式。

Result: 推导出DIGing算法精确最坏情况收敛速率及相应参数的显式公式，数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提框架可精确刻画分布式优化算法的最坏情况收敛性能，提供了系统化设计方法论。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of designing distributed optimization algorithms. Different to existing methods that usually give sufficient conditions and conservative convergence rates, we propose a framework for determining the exact worst-case convergence rate. The worst-case convergence is defined over the set of $μ$-strongly convex and $L$-Lipschitz objective functions and the set of connected graphs with given algebraic connectivity. Focusing on the Distributed Inexact Gradient Tracking (DIGing)--an algorithm widely deployed in applications and extensively studied in recent years, we develop a systematic design methodology by integrating techniques from graph signal processing and robust control theory. First, we present a novel decomposition structure that enables partial subsystem decoupling. Then we derive explicit formulas for the exact worst-case convergence rate and the corresponding parameters. Numerical experiments validate the correctness and effectiveness of our theoretical results.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅建立了原问题与对偶问题最优值相等的值对偶理论，但值相等并不能保证对偶上确界可被达到，因此需要证明对偶势函数的存在性。

Method: 在紧度量空间和连续传输代价假设下，分析固定参考熵正则化重心的连续原-对偶关系，利用指数原-对偶关系恢复最优传输计划。

Result: 证明了连续函数类中对偶最大化势函数的存在性；建立了最优传输计划组与重心的存在性和唯一性；势函数通过指数关系恢复最优计划，并给出重心关于η的显式密度，该密度连续且严格为正；势函数继承代价的正则性，并在自然加性规范变换下唯一。

Conclusion: 结果强化了已知的值对偶理论，为固定参考正则化重心问题提供了完整的连续原-对偶描述。

Abstract: We study the fixed-reference entropy-regularized Wasserstein barycenter problem, in which a probability measure $η$ on the barycenter space is chosen before optimization and the $i$th transport plan is regularized relative to $ν_i\otimesη$. Earlier work established a dual formulation for this model and equality between the primal and dual optimal values. However, equality of values does not by itself guarantee that the dual supremum is achieved. Our main result shows that, on compact metric spaces with continuous transport costs, the dual problem admits maximizing potentials in the original class of continuous functions. For completeness, we also establish existence and uniqueness of the optimal tuple of transport plans and of the barycenter. The maximizing potentials recover the optimal plans through exponential primal--dual relations and give an explicit density of the barycenter with respect to $η$. This density is continuous and strictly positive. We further show that the potentials inherit regularity from the costs and are unique up to the natural additive gauge transformations. These results strengthen the known value-duality theory and provide a complete continuous primal--dual description of the fixed-reference regularized barycenter problem.

</details>


### [89] [Near-Optimal Pure Single-Loop Extragradient Method for Strongly Convex--Strongly Concave Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20327)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般非线性耦合下的光滑强凸-强凹极小极大优化问题，希望设计无需内层求解、精度调度或分阶段重启的简单算法，并达到最优条件数复杂度。

Method: 提出固定参数的纯单环阻尼外推梯度法，每次迭代（初始化后）使用两次全梯度评估，并引入辅助反馈递推，不需要内层求解、精度调度或分阶段重启。

Result: 建立了最后迭代线性收敛，并证明将到鞍点的平方欧氏距离降至初始值的ε倍需要 O(√(κxκy) log(2κxκy/ε)) 次全梯度查询；该界在对数因子内达到最优条件数阶；数值实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该固定显式更新的单环方法在简单性与最优复杂度之间取得良好平衡，适用于确定性无约束强凸-强凹极小极大优化。

Abstract: We study smooth strongly convex--strongly concave minimax optimization with general nonlinear coupling in the deterministic unconstrained setting. We propose a pure single-loop damped extragradient method with fixed parameters and two new full-gradient evaluations per iteration after one initialization query. The method uses an auxiliary feedback recursion and requires no inner solves, accuracy schedules, or staged restarts. We establish last-iterate linear convergence and show that reducing the squared Euclidean distance to the saddle point to an $\varepsilon$ fraction of its initial value requires $O(\sqrt{κ_xκ_y}\log(2κ_xκ_y/\varepsilon))$ full-gradient queries, where $κ_x=L/μ_x$ and $κ_y=L/μ_y$. This bound attains the optimal condition-number order up to logarithmic factors through fixed explicit updates. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the method.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MP-BVCs因失去标准约束资格而难以求解，现有理论仅针对块大小为1的经典MPVC，需扩展约束资格理论并寻找在较弱条件下收敛的有效算法。

Method: 基于经典MPVC理论扩展MPBVC的约束资格理论，证明其LICQ型条件蕴含GCQ；提出分段梯度流方法（经典方法在MPBVC上的推广），在GCQ下保证收敛并直接计算强稳定点。

Result: 在含两个乃至三个施加荷载力的桁架拓扑设计实例上进行数值实验，验证了方法的鲁棒性与有效性。

Conclusion: 所提理论扩展与分段梯度流方法在GCQ条件下有效求解MP-BVCs并直接得到强稳定点，在桁架拓扑设计中表现稳健高效。

Abstract: Mathematical Programs with Blocks of Vanishing Constraints (MP-BVCs) are a challenging class of optimization problems due to the loss of standard constraint qualifications. Based on existing theory for classical MPVCs, i.e., MPBVC with block size one, we extend the constraint qualification theory for MPBVCs and show that the known LICQ-type condition for MPBVCs implies the Guignard Constraint Qualification (GCQ). A novel piecewise gradient flow approach converges under GCQ. It directly computes strongly stationary points and is an extension of the piecewise gradient flow approach for classic MPVC problems. Numerical experiments on several truss topology design instances with two and even three applied load forces demonstrate the robustness and effectiveness of the method.

</details>


### [91] [Geometry and Convergence of Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20400)
*Alberto González-Sanz,Marcel Nutz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为二次正则化最优传输（小正则化ε）建立了尖锐收敛率，量化了正则化最优解支撑的稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二次正则化最优传输在小正则化ε下的收敛行为，并刻画正则化最优解支撑的稀疏结构。

Method: 针对R^d中光滑边际密度与二次代价，分析支撑几何与对偶势，建立对偶势的一致Hessian双侧界并推导均匀收敛率。

Result: 支撑位于Brenier图ε^{1/(d+2)}量级距离内，其每个截面被该量级半径的球夹逼；对偶势满足一致双侧Hessian界，势以ε^{2/(d+2)}、梯度以ε^{1/(d+2)}均匀收敛，速率尖锐。

Conclusion: 所得收敛率是尖锐的，并进一步确定了收敛性失效的正则性阈值。

Abstract: We establish sharp convergence rates for quadratically regularized optimal transport with quadratic cost in the regime of small regularization $\varepsilon$. In particular, we quantify the sparsity of the support of the regularized optimizer. For smooth marginal densities in $\mathbb{R}^d$, this support lies within a distance of order $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$ from the Brenier graph, and every section of the support is sandwiched between balls with radii of that order. The geometry of the support is closely linked to the dual potentials. We show that the potentials satisfy uniform two-sided Hessian bounds and converge uniformly at rate $\varepsilon^{2/(d+2)}$, while their gradients converge at rate $\varepsilon^{1/(d+2)}$. The rates are sharp, and we further identify the regularity threshold where convergence breaks down.

</details>


### [92] [Convergence rate of the moment-SOS hierarchy for univariate polynomial optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20544)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩-SOS（平方和）层级是求解多项式优化问题的常用方法，但其收敛速率（尤其在一元情形、可行集描述含边界退化时）尚缺乏精确刻画。

Method: 结合有限生成一元二次模的结构，构造切比雪夫多项式以近似恢复可行集的自然生成元，同时控制证书的次数；并通过对偶/等价重述（三次一元问题、含尖点奇异的二元POP）进行分析。

Result: 对任意固定的一元POP，松弛误差为O(1/r^2)，其中r为松弛阶；可行集描述的边界退化只影响常数不改变收敛指数。给出一个四次例子，其松弛误差恰为1/(2r(r−1))，证明二次速率为最优。

Conclusion: 一元POP的矩-SOS层级具有二次收敛速率，且该速率在一般情况下是最优的；边界退化不损害收敛阶。

Abstract: We study the convergence rate of the moment-SOS (sum-of-squares) hierarchy for polynomial optimization problems (POPs) on a bounded subset of the real line described by arbitrary polynomial inequalities. We prove that, for every fixed univariate POP, the relaxation error is bounded by $O(1/r^2)$, where $r$ is the relaxation order. In particular, boundary degeneracies in the polynomial description of the feasible set affect the constant but not the convergence exponent. The proof combines the structure of finitely generated univariate quadratic modules with a Chebyshev polynomial construction that approximately recovers the natural generators of the feasible set while controlling the degree of the certificate. We also give an elementary degree-four example for which the relaxation error is exactly $1/(2r(r-1))$, showing that the quadratic rate is optimal. Equivalent reformulations connect this example to a cubic univariate problem and to a bivariate POP whose feasible set has a cusp singularity at the minimizer.

</details>


### [93] [Complexity Of Output Feedback Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20636)
*Amir Ali Ahmadi,Abraar Chaudhry,Ijay Narang,Yukai Tang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明：除非P=NP，线性系统的输出反馈镇定不存在多项式时间（甚至伪多项式时间）算法，在连续与离散时间下均成立，解决了控制论中著名公开难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输出反馈镇定是控制理论中最基础的问题之一，其计算复杂度长期未明，属于著名公开问题。作者旨在判定该问题是否存在高效算法。

Method: 采用计算复杂性理论方法，将输出反馈镇定的求解归约为NP难问题，从而给出基于 P≠NP 假设的不可解性证明；分析覆盖连续与离散时间两类系统及线性控制器。

Result: 除非P=NP，不存在多项式时间或伪多项式时间的输出反馈镇定算法，连续与离散时间均如此；此外构造了一族可镇定系统，任何多项式时间算法都无法用标准表示写出其镇定控制器。

Conclusion: 输出反馈镇定的计算本质是NP难的，否定其高效可解性，为控制理论的计算复杂性研究确立了重要界限。

Abstract: We show that unless P = NP, there cannot be a polynomial-time (or even pseudo-polynomial-time) algorithm for output feedback stabilization of a linear dynamical system with a linear controller. This settles one of the best-known open problems in control theory. The result holds in both continuous and discrete time. We also present a family of stabilizable linear dynamical systems for which no polynomial-time algorithm can write down a stabilizing controller in its standard representation.

</details>


### [94] [The First-Order Oracle Complexity of Lipschitz Convex Optimization in Nondual Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20687)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Brian Bullins,Cristóbal Guzmán,Mathieu Molina*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究 $\ell_p$ 球上关于 $\ell_q$ 范数 Lipschitz 凸目标的一阶黑盒优化，正面回答了 COLT 开放问题（非光滑情形），证明更小可行集（$p<q$）的几何可改进收敛率，率匹配下界（至对数因子）。关键技术是带"最大仿射损失"比较器的新型在线学习博弈，用序列 fat-shattering 维刻画其值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Guz15b 提出的 COLT 开放问题的非光滑版本：更小可行集的几何（$p<q$）能否改进凸优化的收敛率，并推进 Sridharan [Sri12] 提出的关于凸、中心对称可行集与次梯度集合的一般性问题。

Method: 构造新的在线学习博弈，比较器以已观测仿射损失的最大值为准则；用组合在线学习量——序列 fat-shattering 维——对该博弈值给出上下界，并针对 $\ell_p/\ell_q$ 情形刻画该维度。方法适用于凸、中心对称且满足一类 minmax 定理的 $X$ 与 $H$。

Result: 正面解决开放问题，率匹配已有下界（至对数因子）；在 $\ell_1$ 球上对凸、Euclidean-Lipschitz 优化取得 $\widetilde O(1/T)$，优于经典 $O(1/\sqrt T)$。作为几何副产品（独立有趣），给出若干 Banach 几何下凸包到样本均值期望距离的估计——Wendel 定理的定量版本，适用于有界一般分布而非仅中心对称分布。

Conclusion: 更小可行集的几何确实能加速收敛，所提在线学习博弈与 fat-shattering 维框架统一处理了该问题，并推广了 Wendel 型几何结果。

Abstract: We study first-order black-box convex optimization over an $\ell_p$-ball for objectives Lipschitz in the $\ell_q$-norm, solving in the affirmative the nonsmooth version of the COLT open question (Guz15b) on whether the geometry of a smaller feasible set ($p < q$) can improve convergence rates in convex optimization, and matching prior lower bounds up to logarithmic factors. Our rates include \(\widetilde O(1/T)\) for convex Euclidean-Lipschitz optimization over the $\ell_1$-ball, improving on the $O(1/\sqrt{T})$ classical rate under general assumptions. The key technical device is a new online learning game, where the comparator is evaluated using the maximum of affine losses observed so far. We bound the value of this game above and below in terms of a combinatorial online learning quantity: the sequential fat-shattering dimension, which we characterize for the $\ell_p / \ell_q$ case. Our results generally apply when the feasible set $X$ and the set of possible subgradients $H$ are convex, centrally symmetric, and admit a type of minmax theorem, advancing on a fundamental question by Sridharan [Sri12, Section 10.1.2, Q3]. As a geometric consequence of our analysis, of independent interest, we obtain estimates for the expected distance of a convex hull of samples to their mean in several Banach geometries, a version of the celebrated Wendel's theorem (Wen62), but quantitative and for bounded general distributions as opposed to centrally symmetric ones.

</details>


### [95] [Stable Movement for Nondual Lipschitz Convex Optimization: Efficiency and Nearly Optimal Oracle Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20701)
*David Martínez-Rubio,Cristóbal Guzmán*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文为 G-Lipschitz 凸函数在 ℓp 球上、关于 ℓq 范数的优化问题，设计了高效实现近乎最优一阶 oracle 复杂度的算法，解决了 COLT 2015 开放问题的非光滑端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究 ℓp 球上 G-Lipschitz 凸函数关于 ℓq 范数的一阶 oracle 复杂度，特别是 p<q（非光滑）情形；此前的近乎最优速率尚未被高效实现，是 COLT 2015 开放问题的非光滑端。

Method: 将凸 Lipschitz 优化归约为演化 bundle 子水平集上的 chasing nested convex sets 问题：每次查询要么找到低函数值点，要么产生深割并追踪之；通过选择子的稳定性与深割强制移动之间的二分来限制迭代次数。对 RBp^d 的嵌套子集引入新的"稳定中心"概念。

Result: 对 p<q，T 次查询后误差为 Õ_{p,q}(GR/T^{1/p-(1/q-1/2)_+})，高效实现 MBG+26 的近乎最优速率；p=1,q=2 时为 Õ(GR/T)。稳定中心在 ℓq 范数下移动被 Õ_{p,q}(RT^{1-1/p+(1/q-1/2)_+}) 界住，且在高维下近乎最优；Monte Carlo 平均选择子以高概率达到近乎最优速率。

Conclusion: 该算法在实数算术模型下多项式时间可实现，解决了 COLT 2015 开放问题的非光滑端，并给出了近乎最优的一阶 oracle 复杂度。

Abstract: We study efficient algorithms for realizing the first-order oracle complexity of optimization of $G$-Lipschitz convex functions with respect to the $\ell_{q}$-norm over an $\ell_{p}$-ball of radius $R$, where $1\leq p,q\leq \infty$. For $p<q$, we obtain error $\widetilde{O}_{p,q}(GR/T^{1/p-(1/q-1/2)_{+}})$ after $T$ oracle queries, efficiently realizing the nearly optimal rates of (MBG+26), thereby resolving the nonsmooth end of the COLT 2015 open problem (Guz15b). In particular, the rate is $\widetilde{O}(GR/T)$ for Euclidean Lipschitzness over an $\ell_1$-ball of radius $R$ ($p=1,q=2$). Our solution consists of reducing convex Lipschitz optimization to the chasing nested convex sets problem in sublevel sets of an evolving bundle (LNN95; BBE+20): at each query we either find a point with low function value or we produce a deep cut in the current sublevel of the bundle, that we chase. The dichotomy between stability of selectors and forced movement by deep cuts bounds the number of iterations of the algorithm near optimally. For nested subsets of $R B_{p}^{d}$, we introduce a novel notion of stable center whose movement is bounded by $\widetilde{O}_{p,q}(RT^{1-1/p+(1/q-1/2)_{+}})$ in the $\ell_{q}$-norm after $T$ steps, which we show is nearly optimal in high dimensions. A Monte Carlo average of the proposed selector achieves near-optimal rates with high probability and can be implemented in polynomial time for our optimization algorithm in the real-arithmetic model.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.TR'></div>

### [96] [Herding and Liquidity in Order-Book Markets. III. Leverage and the Onset of Endogenous Liquidity Crises under Weak Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20192)
*Jan Novotny*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文用连续双向拍卖市场模型，将"基本面锚定强度"作为连续控制变量，研究无外生冲击下自生流动性危机的产生机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验基本面锚定对订单簿市场稳定性的因果作用，并刻画锚定近乎移除时内生流动性危机的特征。

Method: 构建含锚定噪声交易者、图表派羊群者、受融资约束做市商及受保证金追缴的杠杆持有者的连续双向拍卖市场；以"匹配羊群的杠杆减非杠杆订单簿耗竭增量"孤立危机，分析杠杆-保证金平面。

Result: 削弱锚定使收益率尾部指数从约6.5降至2.3、积分波动自相关约翻倍（但为短记忆）；危机局限于弱锚定与中等羊群的窄区域，杠杆越高危机越深；危机触发由保证金缓冲（而非杠杆本身）主导，杠杆-保证金二维平面塌缩为单缓冲，收紧维持保证金与加杠杆同样不稳定；触发为平滑亚临界交叉，无潜伏尖峰突变。

Conclusion: 基本面锚定是订单簿市场稳定的关键；保证金缓冲是危机触发的主控变量，维持保证金与杠杆可互换影响稳定性。

Abstract: Fundamental-value anchoring of resting liquidity is a causal stabiliser of an order-book market: while the anchor holds, even a heavily leveraged book stays quiet. We take that anchor strength as a continuous control and characterise the endogenous liquidity crises that appear once it is nearly removed. In a single continuous-double-auction market driven by anchored noise traders, chartist herders, a population of funding-constrained market makers, and leveraged holders subject to margin calls, no exogenous shock is injected, so that any crisis is self-generated. Weakening the anchor turns on both classical leverage stylised facts: the return tail exponent falls from about 6.5 to 2.3 and the integrated volatility autocorrelation roughly doubles, although the clustering is short-memory rather than the long-memory of real markets. Isolating the crisis as a matched-herding levered-minus-unlevered book-depletion increment, we find it confined to a narrow region of weak anchor and intermediate herding, in which more leverage always deepens the crisis. Its onset is governed not by leverage alone but by the margin buffer, the price fall a holder can absorb before a forced sale, set jointly by leverage and the maintenance margin: the two-parameter leverage-margin plane collapses, to leading order, onto this single buffer, so that tightening the maintenance margin is as destabilising as raising leverage. The onset is a smooth, sub-critical crossover: the ignition probability rises gradually, a run-length test reveals no latent sharp tipping, and a branching-ratio proxy stays bounded well below unity.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [Aggregating many estimators using estimated weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19415)
*Emmanuel Guerre,Yuting Wang*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究在只有估计权重可用时，对数量递增的一致估计量进行加权平均的问题，涵盖同质性与异质性两种情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当权重为估计值时，权重与估计量的相互作用可能产生偏误，且忽略权重估计会限制可聚合的估计量数量，因此需要新的加权方法来解决推断问题。

Method: 首先分析估计权重与估计量交互产生的特定偏误项；其次提出估计自适应权重方法；最后给出在估计方差下Cochran Q同质性检验有效性的条件。

Result: 估计权重与估计量的交互会生成特定偏误项，限制了忽略权重估计时可聚合的估计量数量；提出的估计自适应权重可实现统一的标准高斯推断，并在同质与异质下均渐近最优。

Conclusion: 估计自适应权重能在同质性和异质性下同时实现渐近最优与标准高斯推断，并明确了Cochran Q检验在估计方差下有效的条件。

Abstract: Consider an increasing number of consistent estimators to be averaged when only estimated weights are available. The underlying parameter of interest can be identical across estimators (homogeneity) or not (heterogeneity). The contribution of the paper is threefold. First, it is shown that the interaction of the estimated weights with the estimators can generate specific bias terms. This constrains the number of estimators that can be aggregated when weight estimation is ignored in inference. Second, the paper proposes estimated adaptive weights, which allow for standard Gaussian inference in a uniform manner and are asymptotically optimal both under homogeneity and heterogeneity. Third, conditions ensuring the validity of the Cochran (1937) Q test of homogeneity with estimated variance are given.

</details>


### [98] [Stable Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19418)
*Harvey Barnhard,Giacomo Opocher,Rahul Singh*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究政策学习算法如何在预期福利与抽样风险之间权衡，提出算法稳定性是刻画该权衡的核心，并提出 policy-vote bagging 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在循证政策制定中，通常只观测一个实验样本，随后将学到的政策建议大规模实施。基于实验数据学到的政策虽在预期福利上表现良好，但实验中的随机抽样可能导致福利结果不佳，因此需要平衡预期福利与抽样风险。

Method: 指出算法稳定性在刻画和权衡该问题中的核心作用：若一个实验单元被替换时政策学习算法的建议保持稳定，则该算法的抽样风险有限。据此提出 policy-vote bagging：在多个子样本上学习处理决策，再将其投票平均为处理概率。

Result: 相对于仅使用一个子样本，跨子样本平均既保持预期福利，又提升风险厌恶研究者的预期效用；并推导了连接估计精度、子样本规模与福利变动的紧界，在 CARA 效用下给出精确保证。

Conclusion: 算法稳定性是理解和应对政策学习中预期福利与抽样风险权衡的关键，policy-vote bagging 通过子样本投票平均有效降低抽样风险。

Abstract: In evidence-based policymaking, typically one experimental sample is observed, then a learned policy recommendation is implemented at scale. Policies learned from the experimental data can perform well in expected welfare, yet random sampling in the experiment can produce recommendations with poor welfare outcomes. In this paper, we ask: how should policy learning algorithms balance expected welfare against sampling risk? Our main contribution is to show that algorithmic stability plays a central role in characterizing and navigating the tradeoff. Intuitively, if a policy learning algorithm's recommendation remains stable when one experimental unit is replaced, then that algorithm has limited sampling risk. We propose a method for policy learning called policy-vote bagging, which learns treatment decisions on many subsamples then averages their votes into treatment probabilities. Relative to using one subsample, averaging across subsamples preserves expected welfare and improves expected utility for a risk-averse researcher. We derive sharp bounds linking estimation accuracy, subsample size, and welfare variation, including an exact guarantee under CARA utility.

</details>


### [99] [Axiomatizing Local Asymptotic Minimax Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19738)
*Ricky Li*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文用决策论方法解决LAM风险两种定义的歧义，公理化刻画广义α-LAM偏好，并通过Sample Uncertainty Aversion axiom唯一选择attainment LAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中LAM风险有两种定义：一种用于经典下界，另一种用于证明这些下界可达。传统效率论证与任意估计量依赖的加权平均一致，无法揭示研究者实际偏好哪种定义。

Method: 采用决策论方法，公理化刻画具有广义α-LAM表示的偏好集合及其constant-weight子集，并引入Sample Uncertainty Aversion axiom。

Result: 广义α-LAM偏好可能违反Monotonicity等基本理性要求；Sample Uncertainty Aversion axiom在constant-weight类中唯一选择attainment LAM。

Conclusion: Sample Uncertainty Aversion在规范上具有吸引力，因此推荐attainment LAM作为LAM风险的函数形式。

Abstract: Local asymptotic minimax (LAM) risk is a foundational efficiency criterion in statistics and econometrics. The literature uses two definitions of LAM risk: one which appears in classical lower bounds and another which appears in arguments establishing attainment of those bounds. Conventional efficiency arguments are consistent with any estimator-dependent weighted average of the two, and consequently do not reveal which of these generalized $α$-LAM risk indices describes researchers' actual preferences. We take a decision-theoretic approach to systematically resolve this ambiguity. We axiomatically characterize the set of preferences with a generalized $α$-LAM representation, and we document that such preferences may violate basic rationality requirements such as Monotonicity. Motivated by this, we axiomatically characterize the subset with constant-weight representations. Within this class, a Sample Uncertainty Aversion axiom uniquely selects attainment LAM. We argue that Sample Uncertainty Aversion is normatively appealing, and we therefore recommend attainment LAM as the functional form for LAM risk.

</details>


### [100] [Counterexamples and Sufficient Conditions: Comments on "Optimally-Transported Generalized Method of Moments"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20260)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文评论Schennach & Starck (2026a)提出的最优传输广义矩估计(OTGMM),给出反例说明其定理2-6在原假设下不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验OTGMM估计量核心理论结果的正确性,指出原文假设不足及证明缺陷。

Method: 构造反例:针对小误差分析(定理2、3的一致性/渐近正态性)与大误差分析(定理4-6的等价性)逐一反驳。

Result: 定理4在标量模型中选取的值不同于唯一OTGMM最小值并违反样本矩约束;过度识别模型下拉格朗日乘子首分量概率极限不一致;误设定下OTGMM总体值依赖传输度量与可调整变量;Assumption 16不蕴含所用矩阵界。

Conclusion: 给出修正矩方程解亦解原约束问题的充分条件,分离出一致性与渐近正态性条件,并用新矩阵条件替代Assumption 16。

Abstract: We comment on the optimally-transported generalized method of moments (OTGMM) estimator proposed by Schennach & Starck (2026a) and give counterexamples to Theorems 2-6 under their stated assumptions. First, the assumptions used in the small-error analysis are insufficient for consistency in Theorem 2 and asymptotic normality in Theorem 3. Next, we consider the large-error analysis, in which Theorem 4 states that the OTGMM estimator is equivalent to a GMM estimator with modified moments. We show that in a scalar model, Theorem 4 selects a value that differs from the unique OTGMM minimizer and violates the OTGMM sample moment restriction. In an overidentified model satisfying the assumptions used in Theorems 5 and 6, the first component of the Lagrange multiplier has different probability limits under the OTGMM estimator and the GMM estimator with modified moments. Under misspecification, the population value selected by OTGMM depends on the transport metric and on which variables may be adjusted. We give a sufficient condition under which solutions of the modified moment equations also solve the original constrained problem at a given parameter value, and separate conditions for consistency and asymptotic normality of the OTGMM estimator. We also show that Assumption 16 does not imply the matrix bound used in the supplemental proofs and replace Assumption 16 with a matrix condition that yields the bound.

</details>


### [101] [Cosine metric as a structural economic similarity measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20424)
*Marina Telezhkina,Dominik Wied*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出以线性模型系数向量夹角余弦度量结构相似性，并开发相应推断方法，应用于1992年新泽西州最低工资改革。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构相似性（经济机制对共同冲击的响应程度）对经济预测、政策迁移等决策很重要。线性模型中两机制相似性可用系数向量夹角几何表示，但缺乏正式的相似性度量与推断工具。

Method: 将系数向量夹角的余弦作为结构相似性度量，并构建对应的统计推断程序。

Result: 应用于1992年新泽西州最低工资改革前后的工资机制：改革虽明显影响了低工资端分布，但连接工人特征与工资的底层机制与无反事实情形没有差异。

Conclusion: 夹角余弦是刻画线性机制结构相似性的简洁有效工具，其推断方法能揭示改革对分布有影响但机制结构不变的证据。

Abstract: Structural similarity, the extent to which economic mechanisms respond alike to common shocks, matters for economic forecasting, policy transfer, and other decisions that rely on evidence from comparable settings. For mechanisms approximated by linear models, this idea has a simple geometric representation: The smaller the angle between their coefficient vectors, the more similarly they respond to the same shock. This paper introduces the cosine of this angle as a measure of structural similarity and develops the corresponding inference procedure. Applied to New Jersey's wage mechanism around the 1992 minimum-wage reform, the approach yields interesting evidence: Although the reform clearly affected the low-wage part of the wage distribution, the underlying mechanism linking worker characteristics to wages was no different from what would have prevailed without it.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Linearity: Semiparametric Solutions to Contamination Bias with Multi-valued Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20473)
*Michel Csillag Finger,Vitor Possebom*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 该文研究非二值处理下污染偏误的半参数解决方案，用样条函数近似条件期望与倾向得分，重分析11项研究中的18个回归，发现参数与半参数估计存在较大点估计差异，但半参数方法未必显著增加统计不确定性，建议研究者检验结论稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深化Goldsmith-Pinkham等(2024)对污染偏误的讨论，在弱函数形式假设下为非二值处理提供更灵活的半参数解决方案。

Method: 用样条函数近似条件期望和倾向得分函数，对三种抗污染偏误估计量的参数与半参数版本进行比较，重分析11项研究的18个回归。

Result: 参数与半参数方法间存在较大点估计差异；采用灵活的半参数解决方案并未实质性增加统计不确定性。

Conclusion: 建议研究者使用针对污染偏误的半参数工具检验其结论的稳健性。

Abstract: We examine semiparametric solutions to contamination bias for nonbinary treatments. Deepening the discussion by Goldsmith-Pinkham et al. (2024), we detail how spline functions approximate conditional expectation and propensity score functions under weak functional-form assumptions. Reanalyzing 18 regressions across 11 studies, we compare standard linear regressions against parametric and semiparametric versions of three contamination-robust estimators. We document large point-estimate discrepancies between parametric and semiparametric approaches and find that adopting flexible semiparametric solutions may not increase statistical uncertainty substantially. We recommend that researchers verify the robustness of their conclusions to the use of semiparametric tools that address contamination bias.

</details>


### [103] [Using machine learning metrics to provide deeper insights into the performance of choice models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20655)
*Lorenzo Muñoz,Stephane Hess,Thomas O. Hancock,Georges Sfeir*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 论文将机器学习中的混淆矩阵概念引入选择建模(CM)的模型性能评估，提出概率版混淆矩阵，从而可在整体拟合(llog-likelihood)之外从备选项级别评估模型预测表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于比较ML与CM方法性能或为ML提升行为洞察，较少进行跨领域知识迁移。CM通常用对数似然等聚合指标评估模型，忽略备选项级别的预测细节；而ML关注误分类与正确分类，提供更细粒度视角。作者希望将ML的评估思想引入CM。

Method: 提出概率版混淆矩阵：对每个观测到的选项，计算模型在所有选择任务中预测各备选项的平均概率。基于该矩阵可计算概率化的ML指标，同时适用于经典选择模型与ML算法，并联合分析整体拟合与备选项级别预测。

Result: 发现具有相似对数似然的模型可能呈现显著不同的混淆矩阵，揭示聚合指标无法捕捉的概率模式；框架可识别模型系统性"混淆"的备选项，显示备选项之间的权衡，并可指导模型设定。样本外评估显示备选项级别预测的变动显著影响预测性能。

Conclusion: 概率混淆矩阵为CM与ML架起评估桥梁，提供比聚合指标更细致的模型诊断，有助于发现备选项权衡、指导模型设定并改进预测，建议在CM中同时报告整体拟合与备选项级别指标。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) techniques are increasingly drawing interest in the choice modelling (CM) field. The focus has primarily been on comparing the performance of these contrasting approaches or on improving behavioural insights for ML techniques, rather than translating ideas from one field into the other. In the present paper, we specifically focus on knowledge transfer from ML into CM in the context of model performance evaluation. In CM, model performance is typically evaluated using log-likelihood and related indicators, which are aggregate fit metrics that focus on overall fit. Conversely, in ML, the focus is on alternative-level misclassifications and correct classifications, which provide a more nuanced view of the results. To bridge these approaches, we explore the use of a probabilistic version of the confusion matrix, which reports the average probability of the model predicting each alternative, conditional on which alternative was observed to be chosen, across all choice tasks. This enables the computation of probabilistic ML metrics for both classic choice models and ML algorithms. We analyse model performance jointly in terms of overall fit and alternative-level predictions. Our findings demonstrate that models with similar log-likelihood can exhibit substantially different confusion matrices, revealing different probability patterns that aggregate metrics cannot capture. This framework identifies where models systematically `confuse' alternatives, highlighting trade-offs between alternatives, and potentially guiding model specification. Furthermore, evaluating these matrices and metrics out-of-sample reveals alternative-level prediction shifts that significantly impact forecasting performance.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [104] [Generative Query Suggestion via Intent Coverage and Query-Level Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19209)
*Xinpeng Liu,Lu Ma,Jiayi Qiao,Mengyu Zhou,Linglong Li,Xiaofeng Bian,Haonan Chen,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出意图驱动的查询建议框架，通过双阶段优化兼顾单查询质量与整个slate的意图覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式查询建议的核心挑战：既要每条查询有用，又要让整个slate覆盖不同意图。

Method: 双阶段优化：1) 意图感知多样性建模，构造意图对齐的SFT数据，用Intent-Aware Diversity Reward优化意图覆盖；2) 查询级信用分配，将单条质量信号分配给对应查询token，同时在整个slate共享slate级多样性信号。

Result: 在大型生产数据集上（含线上A/B测试与离线评估），点击率、查询质量和意图覆盖均提升。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效平衡单查询质量与slate多样性，并在真实系统中验证有效。

Abstract: Generative query suggestion aims to enhance user engagement by anticipating user intents and recommending relevant follow-up queries. A central challenge is to generate slates whose individual queries are useful while the slate covers distinct intents. We propose an Intent-Driven Query Suggestion Framework with dual-stage optimization. First, intent-aware diversity modeling constructs intent-aligned supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data and uses an Intent-Aware Diversity Reward to optimize intent coverage. Second, query-level credit assignment routes individual quality signals to the corresponding query tokens while sharing a slate-level diversity signal across the slate. Experiments on a large-scale production dataset, including online A/B testing and offline evaluation, show improvements in click-through rate, query quality, and intent coverage.

</details>


### [105] [Layer-wise Curriculum Learning for Efficient LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19213)
*Donggeon Lee,Dooyeon Na,Seungmin Oh,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出层级别课程学习（layer-wise curriculum learning）方法，实现高效LLM压缩，通过由易到难的课程学习和特征缓存多线程策略加速并稳定知识蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM压缩中知识迁移计算开销大、收敛慢，且跨层特征错位问题影响效率。需要一种既高效又稳定的知识迁移方法来压缩大模型。

Method: 将整个模型划分为多个由层组成的片段，采用层级别课程学习，从易到难逐步完成知识迁移；基于累积误差现象的理论分析来加速收敛并稳定迁移过程；提出带多线程策略的特征缓存方法，解决跨层特征错位，最大化GPU利用率。

Result: BERT和GPT-2上达到SOTA，GPU内存和训练时间减少超50%；在相同训练时间下，LLaMA系列和Qwen模型上优于其他剪枝方法，GPU内存占用更低。

Conclusion: 层级别课程学习结合特征缓存多线程策略，在多种模型上实现了先进的压缩性能与高计算效率，兼顾低内存和短训练时间。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce layer-wise curriculum learning for efficient LLM compression. The proposed method facilitates the knowledge transfer from the teacher model to the student model, utilizing a curriculum learning approach that begins with easier optimization tasks and progressively tackles harder ones. In order to adopt the layer-wise learning in LLM compression, we partition the whole model into multiple segments consisting of layers, thereby enabling more computationally efficient knowledge transfer for LLMs. Based on our theoretical analysis of cumulative error phenomenon, layer-wise curriculum learning accelerates convergence while stabilizing the knowledge transfer process. In addition, we present a feature caching method with a multi-threading strategy to efficiently address feature misalignment across layers, maximizing GPU utilization. Consequently, our method exhibits advanced model compression performance, as well as high computational efficiency in terms of minimized memory usage and short training hours. Experiments on multiple datasets show that the proposed method achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing GPU memory usage and training hours by more than 50\% on BERT and GPT-2. Moreover, it outperforms the other pruning methods on LLaMA-family and Qwen models under the same training hours, with a lower GPU memory footprint.

</details>


### [106] [Block Parallelism For Efficient Distributed Long-Context Diffusion Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19242)
*Tarun Suresh,Pranshu Chaturvedi,Hangoo Kang,Parth Shroff,Ishan S. Khare,Hermann Kumbong,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出块并行(BP)及上下文分片块并行(CSBP),突破BDLM长上下文训练的通信与显存瓶颈,在256K-1M上下文上实现显著加速且保持训练语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块扩散语言模型(BDLM)结合块间自回归依赖与块内并行去噪,但长上下文训练受限于分布式注意力通信和激活内存。传统上下文并行(CP)按位置切分"干净+污染"序列,需通信共享的干净K/V与块特定的污染K/V及其梯度,开销大。

Method: 观察到BDLM目标可分解到各目标块上,提出块并行(BP):将每个污染块的计算分配到单个rank。进一步提出上下文分片块并行(CSBP):同时将共享的干净序列分片到这些rank上,从而保持污染K/V与梯度本地化、避免复制干净前缀,并保留BDLM训练语义。

Result: 16块H200、256K上下文下,CSBP相比最佳基线吞吐提升1.18-1.45x(SFT)和1.27-1.33x(AR转BDLM),峰值HBM持平或更低;512K全模型加速达1.61x。8块H100上,CSBP使DFlash2投机解码器训练在512K加速2.48x、1M加速7.59x。12小时DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT中,各checkpoint在SWE-bench Verified和Terminal-Bench Lite上通过率均更高。

Conclusion: CSBP提供了一种高效、语义保持的BDLM长上下文分布式训练并行范式,在大规模模型与超长上下文场景下兼具吞吐、显存与质量优势。

Abstract: Block diffusion language models (BDLMs) combine autoregressive dependencies across blocks with parallel denoising within blocks, but long-context training is constrained by distributed attention communication and activation memory. Conventional context parallelism (CP) shards the combined clean-plus-corrupted sequence by position, communicating shared clean K/V together with block-specific corrupted K/V and their gradients. We observe that the BDLM objective separates over target blocks. We introduce block parallelism (BP), a new distributed parallelism dimension that assigns each corrupted-block computation to one rank. To scale BP to long contexts, we introduce context-sharded block parallelism (CSBP), which also shards the shared clean sequence across those ranks. CSBP keeps corrupted K/V and gradients local, avoids replicated clean prefixes, and preserves BDLM training semantics. On 16 H200 GPUs at 256K context, CSBP improves throughput over the best baseline by 1.18-1.45x for supervised fine-tuning and 1.27-1.33x for conversion of autoregressive models to BDLMs, while matching or reducing peak HBM. Full-model speedup reaches 1.61x at 512K. On eight H100 GPUs, CSBP accelerates DFlash2 speculative-decoder training by 2.48x at 512K and 7.59x at 1M. In matched 12-hour DiffusionGemma 26B-A4B SFT runs, CSBP achieves higher pass rates at every trained checkpoint on SWE-bench Verified and Terminal-Bench Lite. Code: https://github.com/ScalingIntelligence/Turbo-dLLM

</details>


### [107] [Randomized SVD Approximations for Spectral Co-Clustering of Word-Document Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19243)
*Fateme Mazdarani,Carlos Toxtli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种用于二分文本数据归一化谱共聚类的随机近似方法，以降低SVD开销，并发现其效果取决于矩阵稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱共聚类依赖SVD，在高维数据上计算昂贵，标准方法开销大，需要更高效的近似方案。

Method: 提出两种随机近似：一是基于随机投影的随机SVD；二是结合部分SVD与逐元素随机采样。适用于文档簇与词簇数量可能不同的归一化谱共聚类。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上，两种方法相较完整SVD基线均降低运行时间，但表现依赖矩阵稀疏性。随机投影更稳定可靠；采样法在较稠密矩阵上更有用，对已稀疏文本数据收益有限。

Conclusion: 谱共聚类的随机近似方法应根据数据底层结构来选择。

Abstract: Spectral co-clustering is a useful tool for discovering latent structure in word-document matrices, but its reliance on singular value decomposition (SVD) can make standard formulations expensive on high-dimensional data. This paper presents two randomized approximations for normalized spectral co-clustering of bipartite text data when the numbers of document and word clusters may differ. The first method uses randomized SVD through random projection, while the second combines partial SVD with element-wise random sampling. Across real-world and synthetic datasets, both methods reduce runtime relative to the full-SVD baseline, but their behavior depends on matrix sparsity. The random projection method is the more reliable approximation across the tested settings, whereas the sampling-based method is most useful on denser matrices and provides limited benefit on already sparse text data. These results show that randomized approximations for spectral co-clustering should be selected according to the underlying structure of the data.

</details>


### [108] [Radio-Frequency Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19279)
*Zhihui Gao,Shi-Yuan Ma,Yiran Chen,Dirk Englund,Tingjun Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出射频卷积神经网络（RF-CNN），利用无线射频中已有的混频器在频域原生执行卷积，实现边缘设备上低功耗CNN推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备（手机、可穿戴、无人机）算力不足以运行现代神经网络，而额外添加边缘加速器又受限于尺寸、重量、功耗和成本（SWaP-C）。作者希望复用设备已有的通信硬件来避免新增计算硬件。

Method: 利用无线射频中普遍存在的混频器在时域做乘法，等价于在频域执行卷积。将多通道卷积映射到不同频率音调上，由无源混频器单次通过完成计算。权重通过无线传输到达，模拟硬件与通信共享。

Result: 实验证明RF-CNN可运行高达2640万参数、九层的深度CNN，涵盖无线信号分类、图像分类和可控图像生成，性能接近全精度。边缘设备仅耗能于数据准备与读出，低至0.72飞焦/乘累加，比额外数字处理器低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 现有无线基础设施可为已连接的数十亿设备带来高效、先进的AI推理能力。

Abstract: Running artificial intelligence (AI) models directly on edge devices such as smartphones, wearables, and drones offers low latency, pervasive scalability, and data privacy, but these devices rarely carry the computing capability that modern neural networks demand. Edge accelerators have been developed in response, yet each adds computing hardware to devices already constrained in size, weight, power, and cost (SWaP-C). An alternative lies in what these devices already carry: the frequency mixer in every wireless radio multiplies signals in time, natively performing convolution in the frequency domain. Here we introduce radio-frequency convolutional neural networks (RF-CNNs), which repurpose existing communication hardware for CNN inference. Multi-channel convolutions are mapped onto frequency tones for a passive mixer to execute in a single pass. We experimentally demonstrate that RF-CNN runs deep CNNs up to 26.4 million parameters and nine layers from classification of wireless signals and images to controllable image generation, close to full-precision performance. Because the weights arrive over the air and the analog hardware is shared with communication, the edge device spends energy only on data preparation and readout-down to 0.72 femtojoules per multiply-accumulate, two orders of magnitude less than it would cost on an added digital processor. These results suggest that deployed wireless infrastructure can bring efficient, state-of-the-art AI inference to the billions of devices it already connects.

</details>


### [109] [Learning-Induced Dynamical Transition in Recurrent Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19288)
*Varun Vaidya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文构建非平衡动力学平均场理论(DMFT)，描述循环神经网络学习过程中从混沌到稳定动力学的转变，并预测学习时间演化与数值模拟定量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环神经网络的学习会重塑其底层动力学，将初始混沌活动转变为稳定的任务依赖行为，但学习过程中的动力学转变机制缺乏理论描述。

Method: 发展非平衡动力学平均场理论(DMFT)来描述学习过程；引入由慢速反馈驱动的有效反馈强度；推导学习过程中的双时间相关函数。

Result: 发现有效反馈强度驱动网络经历混沌到稳定的转变，该转变对应DMFT解的分岔；识别出临界反馈强度和依赖学习率的临界时间；转变源于学习到的反馈结构逐步形变有效动力学景观；理论预测网络输出随训练的时间演化，与数值模拟定量吻合。

Conclusion: 非平衡DMFT成功刻画RNN学习中的混沌—稳定转变，揭示临界反馈强度、临界时间及学习率依赖关系，并得到数值验证。

Abstract: Learning in recurrent neural networks can fundamentally reshape their underlying dynamics, transforming initially chaotic activity into stable task-dependent behavior. We develop a non-equilibrium dynamical mean-field theory(DMFT) to describe this transition during learning. We show that a slow feedback-driven learning process generates an evolving effective feedback strength that drives the network through a transition from chaotic to stable dynamics defined by a bifurcation of the DMFT solution. By deriving the two-time correlation function throughout learning, we identify a critical feedback strength and a corresponding learning rate dependent critical time separating these regimes. The transition arises from the progressive deformation of an effective dynamical landscape by the growing learned feedback structure. Starting from the untrained state, the theory predicts the time evolution of the network output during training and shows quantitative agreement with numerical simulations.

</details>


### [110] [Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes for Privacy-Preserving Modeling of Heterogeneous Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19337)
*Xianjian Xie,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出个性化联邦层次高斯过程pFedHGP，用于异质客户端分布下的概率回归与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分布在各异质客户端上，需在保护数据隐私（不集中原始数据）的同时实现个性化建模与不确定性感知预测。

Method: 将每个客户端隐函数分解为共享全局成分、共享核结构的客户端偏差、灵活局部残差三部分；采用稀疏诱导变量近似与联邦变分推断，原始数据留在本地，服务器仅同步共享成分的低维统计量；提出瞬时线性混合模型视角，将层次结构关联到多输出高斯过程。

Result: 在冲压吨位监测中仅用13.77%的标注周期即实现完美故障分类；在联邦空气质量建模中恢复地理分区，且无需集中站点级时间序列。

Conclusion: pFedHGP能有效在隐私约束下进行个性化联邦概率建模，兼顾共享与个性化信息并支持不确定性决策。

Abstract: We present Personalized Federated Hierarchical Gaussian Processes (pFedHGP) for probabilistic regression and classification when data are distributed across heterogeneous clients. Each client's latent function decomposes into (i) a shared global component, (ii) a client-specific deviation that shares the global kernel structure, and (iii) a flexible local residual. Sparse inducing-variable approximations and federated variational inference keep raw data local while the server synchronizes only low-dimensional statistics for the shared component. Full predictive distributions support uncertainty-aware decisions. In application studies, pFedHGP attains perfect fault classification in press tonnage monitoring using 13.77% of labeled cycles and recovers geographic zones in federated air-quality modeling without centralizing station-level time series. An Instantaneous Linear Mixing Model viewpoint links the hierarchy to multi-output Gaussian processes for correlated sensors.

</details>


### [111] [How to Guide Your Language Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19356)
*Rohit Dilip,Tianrong Chen,Yuyang Wang,David Van Valen,Joshua Susskind,Miguel Angel Bautista*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"probe guidance"，利用冻结扩散模型内部状态构建引导信号，无需额外前向传播，在扩散语言模型上取得SOTA，并揭示autoguidance机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有autoguidance需额外前向传播，且弱模型与强模型需共享相似动态，实现困难；autoguidance机制本身也理解不足。

Method: probe guidance使用已有扩散模型的冻结内部状态构造引导信号，原理类似autoguidance但省去推理时额外前向传播，并能可靠保证弱/强模型动态相似。

Result: 在连续扩散语言模型上，无条件生成达到新SOTA；应用于1.7B扩散语言模型时，多项选择题基准持续提升；通过probe分析发现弱模型须来自低熵训练区域。

Conclusion: 提供改进扩散语言模型的实用方法，并阐明autoguidance的实际机制。

Abstract: We introduce a new method to guide flow matching models. Our approach, which we call probe guidance, uses the frozen internal states of an existing diffusion model to construct a guidance signal. This works using a similar principle as autoguidance, but eliminates the need for an additional forward pass at inference time and provides a reliable path to ensure that the weak and strong model share similar dynamics. We apply and benchmark this method on continuous diffusion language models, where probe guidance sets a new state-of-the-art performance on unconditional generation. When applied to a 1.7B diffusion language model, probe guidance consistently improves on multiple choice question answering benchmarks. Using our probes, we study the traditional autoguidance setting where the strong model is a weak checkpoint, and find that the weak model must come from a low-entropy region of training. These findings both provide a practical way to improve diffusion language models and shed light on the actual mechanism behind autoguidance, which is currently poorly understood.

</details>


### [112] [Smart Insole Human Activity Recognition for Continuous Monitoring in Elderly Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19359)
*Edwin Rios,Antony Garcia,Fengpei Yuan,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种无线智能鞋垫平台及机器学习流程，通过足底压力与惯性信号识别坐、站、走和不稳定行走状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒常伴随移动能力、平衡和姿势转换的变化，需要可穿戴传感手段进行活动监测与跌倒预防。

Method: 每只鞋垫集成16个主动压力传感点与六维IMU（三轴加速度和角速度），采集15名健康成人80Hz数据并做重叠窗口分割；先用分层10折交叉验证筛选窗口长度和候选模型，再用参与者独立的5折分层分组交叉验证获得主要性能；比较HGB与紧凑1D-CNN。

Result: 在参与者独立协议下，HGB对左、右脚分别取得macro-F1 0.954和0.959，双侧感知为0.980；相同折叠下的1D-CNN未显著优于HGB（p=0.0625）。

Conclusion: 低矮鞋垫传感可从压力和IMU测量推断未见过参与者的活动状态，为老年护理中的活动监测和跌倒预防奠定基础。

Abstract: Falls in older adults are often preceded by changes in mobility, balance, and postural transitions. This paper presents a wireless smart insole platform and machine-learning workflow for recognizing sitting, standing, walking, and unstable walking from plantar-pressure and inertial signals. Each insole integrates 16 active pressure-sensing locations and a six-dimensional IMU stream consisting of tri-axial acceleration and angular velocity. Data were collected from 15 healthy adults at 80~Hz and segmented into overlapping windows. Window length and candidate model families were first screened with stratified 10-fold cross-validation; the primary performance estimate was then obtained with participant-independent 5-fold Stratified Group cross-validation, ensuring that all windows from a participant remained in a single fold. Under this protocol, Histogram-Based Gradient Boosting (HGB) achieved macro-F1 scores of 0.954 and 0.959 for the left and right feet, respectively, and 0.980 with bilateral sensing. A compact 1D-CNN evaluated with the same participant-independent folds did not significantly outperform HGB ($p=0.0625$). The results show that low-profile footwear sensing can infer activity state from pressure and IMU measurements for participants unseen during training, establishing a basis for activity monitoring and fall prevention in elderly care.

</details>


### [113] [Stiefel Attention: When the Geometry of Transformer Projection Matrices Dominates Optimizer Choice---and When It Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19363)
*Rubén Darío Guerrero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文将注意力中的查询/键投影约束在Stiefel流形上，并用带信赖域、极式回缩和逐帧标量二阶矩的Riemannian Adam优化，从理论与实验两方面论证其能稳定保留学习到的注意力几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Euclidean优化器对WQ、WK的几何无约束，可能破坏注意力结构；作者想通过流形约束与合适步长规则来保护注意力几何，尤其抵抗权重衰减驱动的坍缩循环。

Method: 将WQ、WK限制在Stiefel流形St(d,r)上；使用Riemannian Adam，每帧维护一个标量二阶矩，以信赖域限制步长，并采用极式回缩。给出四个命题：该更新在嵌入度量下为最速下降、与梯度尺度无关、良条件、且精确O(d)-等变，并用float64数值验证；第五个命题指出权重衰减在Stiefel流形上的Riemannian梯度恒为零。

Result: 模算术grokking中单次运行第20000轮验证准确率97.0%，基线61.1%；CIFAR-10 patches上相对12个配对起点提升+8.98个百分点（t=60.6，12/12），且随数据量增大差距扩大。固定步长Riemannian更新每步移动量比同形AdamW矩阵少24–40倍，直接冻结帧仅损失0.28个百分点；消融显示收益主要来自步长尺度自由，投影器与等变性无可测贡献。

Conclusion: 将注意力投影约束到Stiefel流形并采用该Riemannian Adam，可稳定保存注意力几何并带来性能提升；关键在于自由的步长尺度，而非投影或等变性；权重衰减的零Riemannian梯度解释了机制。同时指出规范移除不能作为方法动机，因为损失不变方向本身没有梯度。

Abstract: The query and key projections $\WQ,\WK$ in attention are almost always trained by Euclidean optimizers with no constraint on their geometry. We constrain them to the Stiefel manifold and optimize them there with a Riemannian Adam that carries one scalar second moment per frame, caps its step by a trust region, and retracts polarly. Four propositions prove this update is steepest descent in the embedded metric, independent of gradient scale, well conditioned, and exactly $\mathrm{O}(d)$-equivariant, each certified numerically in \texttt{float64}. A fifth supplies the mechanism: weight decay has \emph{identically zero} Riemannian gradient on $\St(d,r)$, since $W = W I_r$ lies in the normal space, so the learned attention geometry survives the collapse cycles that decay drives through the rest of the model. On modular arithmetic grokking, a single run holds $97.0\%$ validation accuracy at epoch 20\,000 against the baseline's $61.1\%$---an unstable endpoint we report as evidence for the mechanism rather than as an effect size. On CIFAR-10 patches the same rule gains $\mathbf{+8.98}$\,pp over 12 paired starts ($t{=}60.6$, $12/12$), and the gap widens with data rather than eroding. The step rule earns this: a fixed-step Riemannian update is degree one in the gradient, so it moves $24$--$40\times$ less per step than an identically shaped AdamW matrix---its frames barely leave their initialization, and freezing them outright costs only $0.28$\,pp. An ablation credits the whole gain to making the step scale free, and nothing measurable to the projector or to equivariance. A negative result sharpens the account: gauge removal cannot motivate the method, because a direction along which the loss is invariant carries no gradient at all.

</details>


### [114] [Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) Improves Classification Accuracy of Bacterial Raman Spectral Data Enabling Portable Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19453)
*Kaitlin Zareno,Jarett Dewbury,Siamak K. Sorooshyari,Hossein Mobahi,Loza F. Tadesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出用锐度感知最小化（SAM）优化器提升拉曼光谱病原体分类模型的泛化能力，在临床菌株分类任务中相比Adam最高提升10.5%单次划分准确率，平均提升2.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗菌素耐药性问题严峻（2050年预计年致死1000万人），资源匮乏地区受害最深。拉曼光谱作为快速便携的耐药检测手段很有前景，但现有算法存在两大局限：1）在有限数据集上跨多样患者群体泛化能力差；2）因需处理低质量光谱数据而依赖特征提取等复杂预处理步骤，增加了模型复杂度。

Method: 采用锐度感知最小化（Sharpness-Aware Minimization, SAM）优化器，以提升模型在临床细菌分离株分类任务中跨多种超参数设置的泛化能力。

Result: 相比传统优化器Adam，SAM在光谱分类任务中单次数据划分可提升准确率最高达10.5%，在所有划分上平均准确率提升2.7%。

Conclusion: SAM能够有效增强模型的泛化性能，有望推动AI驱动的拉曼光谱工具在临床中的应用。

Abstract: Antimicrobial resistance is expected to claim 10 million lives per year by 2050, and resource-limited regions are most affected. Raman spectroscopy is a novel pathogen diagnostic approach promising rapid and portable antibiotic resistance testing within a few hours, compared to days when using gold standard methods. However, current algorithms for Raman spectra analysis 1) are unable to generalize well on limited datasets across diverse patient populations and 2) require increased complexity due to the necessity of non-trivial pre-processing steps, such as feature extraction, which are essential to mitigate the low-quality nature of Raman spectral data. In this work, we address these limitations using Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM) to enhance model generalization across a diverse array of hyperparameters in clinical bacterial isolate classification tasks. We demonstrate that SAM achieves accuracy improvements of up to 10.5% on a single split, and an increase in average accuracy of 2.7% across all splits in spectral classification tasks over the traditional optimizer, Adam. These results display the capability of SAM to advance the clinical application of AI-powered Raman spectroscopy tools.

</details>


### [115] [Machine-Learning Assessment of the Predictive Value of Inflammatory Biomarkers for Cognitive Impairment in an Older Hispanic Adult Cohort](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19374)
*Antony Garcia,Gabrielle Britton,Alcibiades Villarreal,Diana Oviedo,Giselle Rangel,Xinming Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在小样本临床表格数据(PARI-HD队列,n=165)上构建了防泄漏的阈值似然朴素贝叶斯分类器,识别出I-309(CCL1)是可解释的认知障碍预测候选特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小样本临床表格数据需要可解释机器学习,因为深度学习不适用、集成模型难以审视;且统计显著性不等于预测价值,需区分二者。

Method: 采用防泄漏的阈值似然Bernoulli/朴素贝叶斯(BNB/CNB)分类器。在每个训练折内,将连续预测变量通过监督式卡方导出离散状态,收入以分类似然进入模型;所有数据依赖步骤均在重复分层10折交叉验证(30次重复)内完成。

Result: 人口学基线ROC-AUC为0.630±0.017。I-309(CCL1)为主要增量特征,AUC提升0.110,配对DeLong检验在100%重复中p<0.05。主分析中DeLong p=0.0018,200次随机划分中位p=0.0011。18个候选标志物中仅I-309通过BH校正(q=0.032,85%随机划分满足q<0.05)。

Conclusion: I-309/CCL1是表格化认知障碍预测的可解释候选特征,尚需外部验证。

Abstract: Small clinical tabular datasets require interpretable machine learning because deep learning is often impractical and ensemble models can be difficult to inspect. A key pitfall is that statistical significance does not necessarily imply predictive utility. Using data from the Panama Aging Research Initiative--Health Disparities (PARI-HD) cohort (n=165), we implemented a leakage-safe threshold-likelihood Bernoulli/Categorical Naive Bayes (BNB/CNB) classifier. Within every training fold, each continuous predictor was reduced to a supervised chi-square-derived state, while income entered the model through a categorical likelihood. All data-dependent steps were performed within repeated stratified 10-fold cross-validation with 30 repeats. The demographic baseline achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.630 +/- 0.017. I-309 (CCL1) was the dominant incremental feature, increasing AUC by 0.110, with paired DeLong tests yielding p<0.05 in 100% of repeats. In the pre-specified primary analysis, I-309 produced a fixed-partition DeLong p=0.0018, with robustness assessed across 200 random partitions, where the median p-value was 0.0011. Within the exploratory family of 18 candidate markers, I-309 achieved a Benjamini-Hochberg-adjusted q=0.032 on the frozen partition and satisfied q<0.05 in 85% of random partitions, whereas no other marker demonstrated reliable incremental predictive value. Because the fitted model is an inspectable table of thresholds and class-conditional probabilities, these results identify I-309/CCL1 as an interpretable candidate feature for tabular prediction of cognitive impairment, pending external validation.

</details>


### [116] [Compressed Active Subspaces for Scalable Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19539)
*Thomas Flynn,Sanket Jantre,Byung-Jun Yoon,Kibaek Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出压缩主动子空间(CAS)，通过结构化等距嵌入将参数映射到压缩空间后构建主动子空间，大幅降低内存需求，实现大模型贝叶斯推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主动子空间方法需存储大量全维模型梯度，随模型规模增大内存开销变得不可行，限制了其在高维大模型中的应用。

Method: 先用结构化等距嵌入将模型参数映射到压缩空间，再在此降维参数化中构建主动子空间，从而减少主动子空间构造所需的内存。

Result: 在规模递增的神经网络上验证了可扩展性，同时保持预测性能与稳健的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: CAS使标准主动子空间方法失效的大型模型也能进行贝叶斯推理，兼顾可扩展性与不确定性量化质量。

Abstract: Active subspace methods provide a framework for quantifying predictive uncertainty in high-dimensional models by identifying and performing inference along parameter directions that have the greatest influence on the model output. However, the construction of active subspaces requires storing many full-dimensional model gradients, which becomes prohibitive as model size increases. We address this limitation by proposing Compressed Active Subspaces (CAS), a scalable approach that first maps the model parameters to a compressed space using a structured isometric embedding and then constructs the active subspace within this reduced parameterization. Our approach substantially reduces the memory required for active subspace construction and enables Bayesian inference for large models where standard active subspace methods become impractical. We demonstrate the scalability of CAS on neural networks of increasing size while maintaining predictive performance and robust uncertainty estimates.

</details>


### [117] [FCx: An algorithm for finding Feasible Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19383)
*Kleopatra Markou,Vana Kalogeraki,Dimitrios Gunopulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FCx方法，通过改进VAE和多因子损失函数，首次高效生成同时具备真实性、低代价和可行性的反事实解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法虽能生成真实、低代价的解释，但往往无法保证可行性，会给出非建设性修改或与未来变化不兼容的建议（如改变种族以获得工作）。

Method: FCx基于改进的变分自编码器（VAE），采用多因子损失函数优化。支持由领域知识用户指定的硬可行约束，以及通过因果推断从数据集中自动推断的软可行约束。代价以数值绝对变化（邻近性）和改变特征数量（稀疏性）衡量，真实性用LOF密度估计衡量，确保反事实位于密集区域。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在多项指标上达到最先进水平，同时保证可行性。

Conclusion: FCx是首个能高效生成兼具真实性、低代价和可行性反事实解释的方法。

Abstract: Counterfactual (CF) explanations identify changes that alter an input's classification. While existing methods produce realistic and low-cost CFs, they often fail to ensure feasibility, by suggesting non-constructive modifications or incompatible with future changes (e.g., changing an individual's race to secure a job offer). We introduce a refinement of CF explanations that explicitly enforces feasibility. Our approach is the first to efficiently generate CFs that are realistic, low-cost and feasible. We accommodate both hard feasible constraints, specified by domain knowledge users, and soft feasible constraints, inferred automatically via causal inference from the dataset. Our method, Feasible Counterfactual Explanations (FCx), is based on a modified Variational Autoencoder (VAE) optimized with a multi-factor loss function. We measure the cost of a change based on the absolute change in values (proximity) as well as the number of features changed (sparsity) while realism is measured based on the LOF for density estimation, guaranteeing that CFs reside in densely populated regions. Extensive experiments on four public datasets show that our approach matches state-of-the-art performance across multiple metrics while guaranteeing feasibility.

</details>


### [118] [Learn Your Own Thoughts: Abstract Token Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19717)
*Khashayar Gatmiry,Avrajit Ghosh,Parsa Mirtaheri,Jason D. Lee,Nika Haghtalab,Emmanuel Abbe,Peter Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出抽象令牌课程（ATC），一种无需监督即可诱导连续中间表征的课程学习框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT依赖对思考令牌的显式监督，需要大量任务特定数据；本文希望无需直接监督或人工设计草稿本即可获得有效连续思考表征。

Method: ATC通过一系列分布在连续表征空间中逐步增加问题复杂度，训练模型发展内部抽象"思考"。

Result: 理论上证明：用ATC训练单层softmax注意力学习parity函数时，注意力自然聚焦于提供"最易路径"的CoT令牌；实验上在图可达性与算术学习任务中验证有效。

Conclusion: ATC为训练连续思考提供了理论与实验支持，优于以往方法，无需人工草稿本设计。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable reasoning capabilities by utilizing chain-of-thought (CoT) as a scratchpad for intermediate stages of thinking. However, CoT techniques require explicit supervision on thinking tokens, which requires rich, task-specific data. In this work, we propose Abstract Token Curriculum (ATC), a novel curriculum learning framework that elicits effective continuous intermediate representations without direct supervision or manual scratchpad design. ATC gradually increases problem complexity through a sequence of distributions, training the model to develop internal abstract ``thoughts'' in the continuous representation space. This paper provides both theoretical and experimental evidence for the benefits of ATC and its advantages over previous methods for training continuous thoughts.
