Trajectory-level analysis explains these effects: context management extends execution trajectories without substantially altering agent behavior, planning changes where trajectories stop, and the action space changes the granularity at which code is written. These findings inform model- and budget-aware harness design and provide a modular framework for evaluating future harness components.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.GN'></div>

# q-fin.GN [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [The Year-End Toll: Frictions Embedded in Option-Implied Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20224)
*Useong Shin*

Main category: q-fin.GN

TL;DR: 期权隐含利率常被视为无摩擦，但本文表明这需要期权方与基准方的"实施楔子"相互抵消。作者用2012-2025年SPX和RUT期权发现，当到期日首次跨越12月31日时，期权融资基差出现2-3个基点的非年化上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献普遍将完成盒式套利（box spread）得到的期权隐含利率视作无风险利率，因盒式组合支付无风险。但作者质疑这一解释：只有在期权侧与基准侧的实施楔子恰好抵消时才成立，否则隐含利率并非经济意义上的"纯净"利率。

Method: 利用2012-2025年的SPX与RUT期权数据，以到期日跨越12月31日（年末）为识别事件，考察期权融资基差的变化。并进行稳健性检验：替代合约与基准、政府债券CIP中的独立证据、以及独立构建的期权面板复现。

Result: 到期日首次跨越12月31日时期权融资基差上升2-3个基点（非年化）；该效应表现为固定价格楔子，在2010年代中期增强，在替代合约与基准下依然存在，在政府债券CIP中独立出现，并在独立构建的期权面板中复现。

Conclusion: 看跌看涨平价（put-call parity）能够精确识别贴现率，但无法确立其经济上的"纯净性"；隐含利率受实施楔子影响，不应简单视为无摩擦利率。

Abstract: Option-implied rates are often treated as frictionless because completed boxes deliver riskless payoffs. I show that this interpretation requires option- and benchmark-side implementation wedges to offset. Using SPX and RUT options from 2012-2025, I find a 2-3 bp unannualized increase in the option funding basis when maturity first crosses December 31. The effect behaves as a fixed price wedge, strengthens in the mid-2010s, survives alternative contracts and benchmarks, appears independently in government-bond CIP, and is reproduced in an independently constructed option panel. Put-call parity can identify discount rates precisely without establishing their economic purity.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [301] [Equivalent-Agent Guidance for Cooperative UAV Payload Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19312)
*Saurabh Kumar,Shashi Ranjan Kumar,Abhinav Sinha*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种双无人机协同搬运刚性负载至静止/机动着陆平台的制导框架，采用虚拟等效代理建模、固定时间滑模制导及链路姿态控制与分配，实现规定时间收敛、鲁棒着陆与实时计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双无人机协同搬运刚性负载至着陆平台（尤其机动平台）面临终端可行性条件、目标机动不确定性和实时计算约束等挑战，需建立统一制导框架以保证收敛性与精度。

Method: 引入虚拟等效代理描述刚性耦合无人机-负载系统平动，以相对距离与视线动力学建模；几何分析给出终端可行性条件；设计固定时间鲁棒滑模制导律调节距离与视线，配合独立链路姿态控制器与控制分配将虚拟指令映射到各无人机输入。

Result: 静止平台可实现任意规定接近角；机动平台零相对速度着陆需终端速度与航向同步且着陆角为零。仿真验证不同接近角与平台机动下精确送达；Raspberry Pi 处理器在环实验满足实时计算要求；对比显示跟踪精度更高、滑模面收敛更快且各无人机控制能量显著更低。

Conclusion: 所提框架保证在一致有界时间内收敛至期望着陆构型，不受初始几何影响，并显式适应目标机动不确定性，兼具精度、能效与实时性优势。

Abstract: This paper develops a guidance framework for cooperative transportation of a rigid payload by two uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) to stationary and maneuvering landing platforms. A virtual equivalent-agent representation is first introduced to describe the translational motion of the rigidly coupled UAV-payload system, allowing the transportation problem to be formulated in terms of relative range and line-of-sight dynamics with respect to the landing platform. A geometric analysis establishes the terminal feasibility conditions for payload delivery. In particular, an arbitrary prescribed approach angle can be achieved for a stationary platform, whereas successful delivery to a maneuvering platform with zero relative velocity requires terminal velocity and heading angle synchronization and consequently a zero landing angle. Leveraging this framework, a robust fixed-time sliding mode guidance strategy is developed to regulate both relative range and line-of-sight dynamics. A separate link-orientation controller and control allocation scheme is presented to map virtual equivalent agent commands to the individual UAV's control inputs. The proposed strategy guarantees convergence to the desired landing configuration within a uniformly bounded time, independent of initial engagement geometries, while explicitly accommodating uncertainties arising from target maneuvers. Numerical simulations demonstrate accurate delivery under different terminal approach angles and platform maneuvers, while processor-in-the-loop implementation on a Raspberry Pi demonstrates that the guidance algorithm satisfies the real-time computational requirements. Nonetheless, a comparative analysis shows that the proposed framework achieves better tracking accuracy and faster sliding surface convergence while requiring significantly less control energy from each UAV.

</details>


### [302] [Optimal Control and Closed-Loop Stability of Droplet Transport in a Microchannel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19320)
*Rajneesh Anand,Mayuresh V. Kothare*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文用最优控制框架研究微通道内液滴输运，结合ODE降阶模型（PMP）与全PDE模型（CMA-ES），揭示两种最优输运策略，并用控制Lyapunov函数证明闭环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限几何中液滴的高效输运对工业应用（如微流控）很重要，需要设计最优控制策略来最小化粘性耗散、实现液滴运动的最优操控。

Method: 基于润滑理论建立微通道液滴输运问题。采用两条互补建模路径：一是降阶ODE模型，用庞特里亚金最大值原理（PMP）优化；二是全非线性PDE模型，用协方差矩阵自适应演化策略（CMA-ES）优化。参数化目标位移、液滴尺寸与毛细数。此外，将控制Lyapunov函数（CLF）框架适配到降阶动力学，分析闭环稳定性。

Result: 发现两种不同的最优输运机制：短距离的"平移-松弛"策略与长距离的"压缩-平移-松弛"策略。表面力与累积粘性耗散的竞争决定了最优策略。降阶控制器可迁移到连续模型的位移范围由毛细刚度决定。证明终止代价（惩罚对目标的偏差）是物理域上的cost-compatible CLF，且CLF兼容反馈能指数稳定目标状态。

Conclusion: 该工作为微通道液滴输运提供了从降阶到全模型的最优控制理论与闭环稳定性保证，揭示了毛细刚度对控制器迁移范围的控制作用。

Abstract: Understanding the efficient transport of fluid droplets in confined geometries has been a domain of interest for industrial applications in recent times. In the present work, we focus on designing control strategies that optimally steer droplet motion. Here, we apply optimal control framework to the droplet transport problem in a microchannel based on lubrication theory that minimizes viscous dissipation. Two complementary modeling routes are adopted: a reduced-order ordinary differential equation (ODE) model optimized via Pontryagin's Maximum Principle, and a full nonlinear partial differential equation (PDE) model optimized using a Covariance Matrix Adaptation-Evolutionary Strategy. By parameterizing target displacement, droplet size, and capillary number, we uncover two distinct optimal transport regimes: a "translate-relax" strategy for short distances and a "compact-translate-relax" strategy for longer targets. In continuum mechanics, the competition between surface forces and cumulative viscous dissipation decides the optimal transport strategies. We further show that the displacement range over which the reduced-order controller transfers to the continuum model is governed by capillary stiffness. We finally address closed-loop stability by adapting a control Lyapunov function (CLF) framework to the reduced-order dynamics. We demonstrate that the terminal cost which penalizes deviation from the target, serves as a cost-compatible CLF on the physical domain, and prove that the CLF-compatible feedback exponentially stabilizes the target state.

</details>


### [303] [Resilient Design and Optimal Operation of Battery Energy Storage Systems for Behind-the-Meter Data Center Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19342)
*Hamed Haggi,Chinmay Morankar*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个高保真超大规模数据中心负荷建模框架，并基于此开发多阶段优化框架，用于BTM数据中心微电网中BESS的规划与韧性设计，协同优化负荷侧与发电侧BESS及多种发电资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大规模数据中心负荷随机性强、波动剧烈，会对发电资产（如燃气轮机）造成运行压力，引发SSTI、轴疲劳、跳机等问题，需在规划阶段兼顾成本、可靠性与韧性。

Method: 构建将设施需求分解为IT与非IT组件、支持秒级与分钟级分辨率的高保真负荷建模框架；在此基础上提出多阶段优化框架，在负荷侧部署BESS作为动态缓冲，在发电侧优化配置BESS提供旋转备用，并与光伏、风电、天然气燃料电池、简单循环及联合循环燃气轮机协同优化，纳入运行、可靠性、韧性与技术特定约束。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法能够识别出经济有效、具备韧性且运行可行的设计方案，满足下一代超大规模数据中心的高可靠性要求并应对能源市场挑战。

Conclusion: 该框架为投资者、开发商与系统运营商提供了面向超大规模数据中心微电网的BESS规划洞见，兼顾经济性与韧性。

Abstract: This paper proposes a high-fidelity hyperscale data center load modeling framework that disaggregates facility demand into IT and non-IT components and represents load behavior at second- and minute-level resolutions. Building upon this foundation, a comprehensive multi-phase optimization framework is developed for the planning and resilient design of BESS for BTM data center microgrids. The framework deploys BESS on the load side to act as a dynamic buffer between stochastic demand and generation assets. By smoothing the load spikes and excursions before they are observed by gas turbines, the load-side BESS reduces operational stress and limits conditions that can contribute to SSTI issues, shaft fatigue, system trips, equipment damage, and forced outages. Simultaneously, generation-side BESS resources are optimally sized to provide spinning reserves and enhance system resilience. The proposed methodology co-optimizes BESS with solar, wind, natural gas fuel cells, simple-cycle gas turbines, and combined-cycle gas turbines while incorporating operational, reliability, resiliency, and technology-specific constraints. Simulation results demonstrate the proposed methodology's ability to identify cost-effective, resilient, and operationally feasible designs that satisfy the demanding reliability requirements and energy market challenges of next-generation hyperscale data centers while providing planning insights for investors, developers, and system operators.

</details>


### [304] [PowerModels-ACOPF-AI: On-the-Fly Machine Learning Approach for Solving AC Optimal Power Flow Integrating Renewable Energy Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19360)
*Bhuban Dhamala,Jose Tabarez,Anup Pandey*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出PowerModels-ACOPF-AI，一种两阶段贝叶斯神经网络代理模型，用于快速、带不确定性预测AC-OPF解，并具备在线自适应再训练机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代电力系统因高可再生能源渗透、负荷波动和运行不确定性而日益复杂，传统AC-OPF优化方法虽精确但可扩展性差、计算负担重，难以实时应用于大电网。

Method: 两阶段贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）代理，预测发电机设定点、母线电压和相角并量化不确定性；集成性能敏感的在线学习机制，识别预测精度下降区域，并调用PowerModels.jl生成额外AC-OPF解进行动态再训练，形成自适应闭环。

Result: 在30节点、200节点和500节点基准系统上验证，展现良好泛化能力，能高效处理随机可再生能源注入，并提供快速、不确定性感知的预测。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为系统操作员的实时辅助工具及传统求解器的快速初始化器，支持未来电力系统的弹性高效运行。

Abstract: The increasing complexity of modern power systems, driven by high renewable penetration, load variability, and operational uncertainty, demands fast and reliable solutions to the AC optimal power flow problem (AC-OPF). Traditional optimization methods, though accurate, often struggle with scalability and high computational burdens, making them impractical for real-time use in large networks. This paper introduces PowerModels-ACOPF-AI, a two-stage Bayesian Neural Network (BNN) surrogate designed to predict generator set points, bus voltages, and phase angles with uncertainty. The framework integrates a performance-sensitive on-the-fly learning mechanism that identifies regions of degraded prediction accuracy and dynamically retrains with additional AC-OPF solutions generated by PowerModels.jl. This self-adaptive loop ensures robust performance, enabling the model to maintain accuracy under novel or highly variable operating conditions. Validation on benchmark test systems of different sizes, namely the 30-bus, 200-bus, and 500-bus networks, demonstrates strong generalization capability, efficient handling of stochastic renewable injections, and the ability to provide rapid, uncertainty-aware predictions. Beyond predictive accuracy, the proposed approach offers practical value as both a real-time advisory tool for system operators and a fast initializer for conventional solvers, thus supporting resilient and efficient grid operation in future power systems.

</details>


### [305] [Feasibility and Singularity in High-Order Safety-Critical Control for Quadrotor UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19362)
*Omayra Yago Nieto,Leonardo Colombo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究有界输入与两两避碰约束下四旋翼编队的高阶安全临界控制，通过有效性/可行性度量刻画缺陷，提出力矩感知动态扩展与GP学习的高阶HOCBF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平方距离障碍在相对位移与推力方向正交时可能失去推力有效性，而非奇异约束在共享输入边界下仍可能联合不可行；需同时解决有效性与聚合可行性问题。

Method: 引入两两有效性与聚合可行性度量；采用力矩感知动态扩展，将姿态力矩显式引入四阶障碍函数，避免正推力下扩展输入行消失；用高斯过程直接学习四阶HOCBF残差，无需对未知扰动求导即可获得鲁棒裕度；构建QP控制器。

Result: 在残差界与持续可行性假设下，所提QP保证避碰，并在标称输入满足鲁棒安全与作动器约束时恢复该标称输入。

Conclusion: 该方法将有效性与可行性分析、动态扩展与GP残差学习结合，为有界输入下四旋翼编队提供具有理论保证的高阶安全临界控制。

Abstract: We study high-order safety-critical control of quadrotor teams under bounded inputs and pairwise collision-avoidance constraints. Squared-distance barriers may lose thrust effectiveness when the relative displacement is orthogonal to the available thrust directions, while nonsingular constraints may still be jointly infeasible under shared bounds. We characterize both phenomena through pairwise effectiveness and aggregate feasibility measures. A torque-aware dynamic extension exposes attitude torques in a fourth-order barrier and prevents the extended-input row from vanishing under positive thrust. Gaussian processes directly learn the fourth-order HOCBF residual, providing robust margins without differentiating unknown perturbations. Under residual-bound and persistent-feasibility assumptions, the resulting QP guarantees collision avoidance and recovers the nominal input whenever it satisfies the robust safety and actuator constraints.

</details>


### [306] [Data-Driven Compositional Safety Verification of Interconnected Monotone Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19396)
*Amirreza Alavi,Majid Zamani,Saber Jafarpour*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种样本高效的组合式形式化安全验证方法，利用局部子系统的单调性，在无需显式模型的情况下构造可处理的局部区间屏障证书，并组合成整体系统的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动方法要么缺乏形式化安全保证，要么依赖基于Lipschitz的稠密状态空间离散化，导致计算开销大、可扩展性差。

Method: 在局部层面，仅用子系统边界评估，借助单调神经网络从局部状态与内部输入空间划分诱导的边界样本中学习区间屏障证书；在全局层面，利用互联结构组合这些局部神经区间屏障证书，并在适当结构假设下将全局安全条件重写为可扩展形式，直接纳入神经网络损失以在局部训练中强制整体安全。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法有效且具备良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了无需显式模型、样本高效的互联单调系统形式化安全验证，兼顾形式化保证与可扩展性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a sample-efficient compositional method for formal safety verification of interconnected monotone systems without requiring explicit models of the local subsystems. Existing data-driven approaches either lack formal safety guarantees or rely on dense Lipschitz-based discretizations of the state space to provide such guarantees, which leads to significant computational overhead and limits scalability. In contrast, we leverage the monotonicity of local subsystems to construct tractable local interval-barrier certificates using only boundary evaluations of each subsystem in a decentralized manner, together with a global condition that guarantees the safety of the overall interconnected system. At the local level, our framework learns interval-barrier certificates using monotone neural networks from boundary samples induced by partitions of the local state and internal-input spaces. At the global level, it composes these local neural interval-barrier certificates using the interconnection structure to certify the safety of the overall system. Furthermore, under appropriate structural assumptions, we reformulate the global safety condition into a scalable form that can be directly incorporated into the neural network loss. This enables the enforcement of overall system safety during local training. The experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [307] [Demystifying Linear Operator Learning for Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19428)
*Max Beier,Nicolas Hoischen,Sandra Hirche,Petar Bevanda*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出用半群框架刻画控制系统算子结构，并用逆问题框架分析学习算法，进而推导出可证明更优的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制系统中的算子演化需要合适的结构性假设与学习理论分析，现有方法往往隐式假设有界算子以获得矩阵表示，缺乏统一的理论框架来比较和设计算法。

Method: 借用(半)群框架描述演化方程中的算子结构，并将学习算法置于逆问题框架下分析，揭示误差分解、收敛保证与最优正则化，从而比较现有方法；研究范围限定为希尔伯特空间上的有界算子。

Result: 通过误差分解、收敛保证和最优正则化揭示了学习模型与数据的关系，能够比较现有方法并推导出可证明有优势的算法；并给出一个针对时变系统的收敛估计器作为框架有效性的验证。

Conclusion: (半)群与逆问题框架为控制系统算子的数据驱动学习提供了统一的结构性与学习理论视角，有界算子假设在实践中并不算过度限制，且能催生收敛的时变系统估计器。

Abstract: This paper proposes a structured approach to learning linear operators for control systems from data. We address both structural and learning-theoretic aspects of the problem. To derive structural assumptions, we propose using the well-established framework of (semi)groups for evolution equations, as operators in control systems are of the same type. Further, we propose analyzing learning algorithms through the lens of the inverse problems framework. This reveals how a learned model depends on the data via error decompositions, convergence guarantees, and optimal regularization -- enabling us to compare existing methods and derive provably advantageous algorithms. In order to obtain these results, we restrict our scope to bounded operators on Hilbert spaces. Although this may appear restrictive, existing approaches often make this assumption implicitly to obtain matrix-like representations. We demonstrate the power of using these frameworks by deriving a convergent estimator for time-varying systems.

</details>


### [308] [Bifurcation Beyond Surface-Tangential Asymptotic Convergence in Continuous Sliding Mode Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19682)
*Fan Zhang,Jingwen Xu,Peng Li,Jun Zhou,Yaohua Guo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文对二阶系统在线性滑模控制下的相位平面轨迹收敛方向进行严格分析，揭示渐近状态比存在分岔，收敛方向未必切线于设计滑模面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献长期依赖相位图直观约定，认为二阶连续SMC下轨迹沿设计滑模面的切线方向趋近平衡点，但该"切线收敛假设"缺乏严格验证，可能掩盖真实的终端几何特性。

Method: 对双积分系统施加标准线性SMC，进行严格的相位平面分析；推导收敛比的闭式表达式，建立分类框架；并将分析扩展至经典PD控制。

Result: 渐近状态比不唯一，而是随控制增益、滑模面参数及初始条件发生分岔；系统可能沿隐含的次级流形收敛而非设计滑模面，表明平滑进入并非全局保证；PD控制中分岔阈值与临界阻尼边界重合。仿真验证了分类结果。

Conclusion: 该研究打通了终端几何与收敛平滑性的联系，给出了预测性框架，可用于高性能运动控制设计，并纠正了对滑模切线收敛的普遍误解。

Abstract: For second-order systems under continuous sliding mode control (SMC), the literature has long relied, largely through phase-portrait illustrations, on the implicit convention that the phase-plane trajectory approaches the equilibrium along a direction tangential to the designed sliding surface. This paper investigates this tangential convergence assumption through a rigorous phase-plane analysis of the double-integrator system subject to standard linear SMC. We reveal that the asymptotic state ratio is not unique but instead exhibits a bifurcation that depends on the control gains, the sliding surface parameter, and the initial conditions. The key finding is that the system may converge along an implicit secondary manifold rather than aligning with the designed sliding surface, implying that smooth entry is not globally guaranteed. We derive closed-form expressions for the convergence ratios and establish a classification framework that precisely characterizes when the smooth-entry assumption holds and when it fails. The analysis is further extended to classical PD control, and we show that the bifurcation threshold coincides with the critical damping boundary that separates the two convergence regimes. These findings bridge terminal geometry and convergence smoothness, providing a predictive framework for high-performance motion control design. Simulation results validate the proposed classification of convergence regimes.

</details>


### [309] [Design of Economic Dispatch Schemes of An Isolated BESS Network Based on Distributed Discrete-time PI+Rest Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19804)
*Yalin Zhang,Zhongxin Liu,Zengqiang Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对孤立电池储能系统(BESS)网络,提出两种基于离散时间动力学的分布式经济调度(ED)方案,引入带重置机制的PI协议(PI+R),实现最优输出功率分配与供需平衡,并兼顾动态线损和容量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孤立BESS网络中,电池单元的容量退化和功率损耗会增加运行成本,需要有效的经济调度方案来降低损耗并保证供需平衡;同时传统方法在共识速率和控制精度上存在不足。

Method: 设计两种分布式ED方案,均包含边际成本(MC)共识控制器与平均功率失配估计器,二者相互耦合。核心是引入带重置机制的PI+R协议:当比例项过零时将积分项重置为0,以加快收敛并减少超调。两种方案的差别在于一个将估计的平均功率失配反馈项纳入闭环误差,另一个不纳入。文中分析了重置机制的有效性、工作条件及稳定性。

Result: 仿真实验表明,设计的第二种方案在共识速率、收敛速度、BESS/智能体即插即用、负载切换及广域系统应用等方面显著优于既有方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的PI+R协议分布式经济调度方案能有效提升孤立BESS网络的调度性能与鲁棒性,其中第二种方案综合表现最优。

Abstract: Battery energy storage systems (BESSs) are widely integrated into smart grids. For an isolated BESS network, however, capacity degradation and power loss of battery units increase operating costs. To alleviate this problem, two distributed economic dispatch (ED) schemes with discrete-time dynamics are developed in this paper, thus obtaining the optimal output power vector and ensuring supply-demand balance while considering dynamic line loss and capacity constraints. It is worth mentioning that proportional-integral protocols with reset mechanisms (PI+R protocols) are introduced into these schemes which can greatly improve the consensus rate and control accuracy. Specifically, a marginal cost (MC) consensus controller and an average power mismatch estimator are the core components of each scheme, where these two are coupled to each other. In this regard, the difference between the two schemes is that for the MC consensus controllers, one involves incorporating the estimated average power mismatch feedback term into the closed-loop system error to design the PI+R protocol, while the other does not. In addition, regarding to each PI+R protocol, the integral term is reset to 0 when the proportional term experiences zero crossing, in order to accelerate the convergence rate and reduce overshoot. The effectiveness, conditions under which the reset mechanism works, and stability of these schemes are all well analyzed. Finally, some simulation cases are designed and compared with an existing solution. From the simulation results, it can be seen that the designed second scheme greatly improves the performance of the previous scheme in consensus rate, convergence rate, BESS/agent plug and play, load switching, and wide area system application.

</details>


### [310] [On unified asymmetric barrier Lyapunov functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19886)
*Susmitha T Rayabagi,Shashi Ranjan Kumar,Debasattam Pal,Dwaipayan Mukherjee*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的非对称障碍Lyapunov函数（BLF），泛化了对数对称BLF，无需不连续切换函数，用于SISO非线性系统的输出约束控制，实现指数快速跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对称障碍Lyapunov函数普遍依赖不连续切换函数，导致设计复杂、不统一。作者希望提出一个统一、平滑的障碍Lyapunov函数，同时涵盖对称与非对称输出约束情形。

Method: 提出一个统一的非对称障碍Lyapunov函数，证明其平滑性且无需切换函数。基于该函数为一类带输出约束的SISO非线性系统设计控制律，并构造对称class K函数对该BLF进行上下界约束，以建立局部指数收敛性。

Result: 所提控制律实现指数快速输出跟踪，统一了对称与非对称输出约束的控制设计与结构；该BLF可被对称class K函数上下界约束，配合控制律实现局部指数收敛。数值算例验证性能，并与现有对数BLF进行对比。

Conclusion: 提出的统一非对称BLF简化了约束控制设计，消除了对不连续切换函数的需求，并可统一处理对称与非对称输出约束下的指数跟踪控制。

Abstract: Barrier Lyapunov functions (BLFs) have been a popular choice when dealing with constrained control problems. In the current article, we present a unified asymmetric barrier Lyapunov function that generalizes the existing logarithmic symmetric Lyapunov function. We show that the proposed function is smooth and does not require the discontinuous switching function that is ubiquitous in the asymmetric barrier Lyapunov functions existing in the literature. Based on the proposed barrier Lyapunov function, a control law, guaranteeing exponentially fast output tracking, is designed for a class of single-input single-output nonlinear systems with output constraints. We show that the proposed control law unifies the control design and structure for a system with either symmetric or asymmetric output constraints. Furthermore, we constructively show that the proposed function can be bounded from above and below by symmetric class $\mathcal{K}$ functions which help in establishing local exponential convergence together with the proposed control law. Lastly, we provide numerical examples to illustrate the performance of the proposed control and compare the results with the existing logarithmic BLFs.

</details>


### [311] [Foldable Antenna Array in Space-Air-Ground Integrated Networks: Architectures and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20011)
*Jikang Deng,Ki-Hong Park,Mohamed-Slim Alouini*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文综述可折叠天线阵列(FAA)的基本原理、架构及其在天地一体化网络(SAGIN)中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAGIN整合了覆盖、移动性和载荷受限的异构平台，对灵活天线架构有强烈需求。FAA通过可控关节重构阵列几何、位置和朝向，是有前景的方案，但现有研究仅考虑简单折叠配置且部署收益有限，FAA架构及其在SAGIN中的潜力尚未充分探索。

Method: 文章介绍FAA的基础与架构（折叠机制、可折叠层级、功能能力），并分析其在地面基站、低空平台、高空平台和卫星中的应用及挑战与解决方案，最后给出案例研究。

Result: 通过案例研究展示了FAA在SAGIN中带来的通信性能提升。

Conclusion: 文章指出开放问题与未来研究方向，论证FAA是提升SAGIN通信性能的有前景技术。

Abstract: Space-air-ground integrated networks (SAGINs) integrate heterogeneous platforms with different coverage, mobility, and payload constraints, which creates strong demands for flexible antenna architectures. Foldable antenna arrays (FAAs) provide a promising solution by reconfiguring array geometry, antenna positions, and orientations through controllable joints or hinges. However, existing studies on FAAs have mainly considered simple folding configurations and limited deployment benefits, while FAAs' architectures and broader potential for SAGIN remain largely unexplored. In this article, we present the fundamentals and architectures of FAA, including folding mechanisms, foldability levels, and functional capabilities. We then examine FAA applications for ground base stations, low-altitude platforms, high-altitude platforms, and satellites, together with their potential challenges and solutions. Finally, we highlight open issues and future research directions, and provide a case study to demonstrate the FAA-enabled communication performance improvement in SAGIN.

</details>


### [312] [The Small-Talk Effect in Practical Synchronization of Heterogeneous Oscillating Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20115)
*Finn Voland,Vincent Schmidtke,Zonglin Liu,Olaf Stursberg*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究异构Lienard振子同步中的多时间尺度通信方案，通过全局更新与局部频繁交互互补，实现通信率与同步性能的灵活权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖所有网络系统在离散时刻进行全局信息交换，难以灵活调整通信率与同步性能之间的权衡。

Method: 提出一种在子网络内以更频繁的局部交互补充全局更新的方案；针对给定同步误差界，推导可选取全局与局部更新率作为设计参数的条件。

Result: 数值示例表明局部交互具有有益效果，可改善同步性能。

Conclusion: 多时间尺度通信方案为异构Lienard振子同步提供了通信率与性能权衡的设计灵活性，局部交互可有效补充全局更新。

Abstract: This paper investigates multi-time scale commu- nication schemes in synchronization of heterogeneous Lienard oscillator systems. Existing results rely on global exchange of information for all networked systems at discrete time instants, providing limited flexibility in adjusting the trade-off between communication rate and synchronization performance. A scheme to investigate how global updates are complemented by more frequent local interactions within a sub-network is thus proposed in this paper. For a prescribed bound on the synchronization error, conditions are derived under which the global and local update rates can be selected as design parameters. A numerical example illustrates the beneficial effect of the local interactions.

</details>


### [313] [Sparse One-Step-Ahead Optimal Control of Time-Varying Affine Opinion Networks: Tracking and Competitive Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20450)
*Gabriel Gentil,Amit Bhaya*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究时变意见网络中资源受限的外部影响：控制器每步选一小撮智能体并施加标量干预。将一步问题化为基数约束支撑目标，用一次排序即得精确最优解；周期仿射动力学下给出基于环的终极界与不变目标精确跟踪，并证明多玩家博弈为势博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意见网络中的外部影响通常受资源限制，控制器既要选谁（稀疏支撑）又要定干预强度（标量），二者耦合使最优控制与博弈分析困难。论文旨在刻画这种"选人+选值"的联合决策结构，给出可精确求解、可分析跟踪与竞争性质的框架。

Method: 采用仿射自由响应（涵盖DeGroot与Friedkin-Johnsen动力学）后接稀疏直接动作。消去标量动作，将一步问题归约为基数约束支撑目标，其全局最优由最大或最小残差分量给出，故精确稀疏一步最优控制只需一次排序、O(|T| log|T|)。对周期仿射动力学推导状态依赖的低秩反馈，得到基于环的终极界与模型不变目标的精确跟踪；多外部玩家时，各稀疏最优响应保持相同排序结构，完整二元-连续阶段博弈为精确势博弈。

Result: 给出精确稀疏一步最优控制的排序算法与复杂度；周期仿射下建立环基终极界与不变目标跟踪条件，DeGroot/Friedkin-Johnsen特例揭示目标不变性与仿射失配项作用；多玩家场景证明每冻结状态存在纯策略纳什均衡，并用同态福利基准区分标量竞争损失与策略支撑选择额外损失。数值实验验证精确支撑选择，展示切换顺序效应、跟踪、资源权衡与竞争实现。

Conclusion: 该框架将资源受限意见控制统一为可精确求解的稀疏优化与势博弈问题，兼具计算可处理性与跟踪/均衡保证，为多智能体竞争影响提供理论基准与算法基础。

Abstract: This paper studies resource-limited external influence in time-varying opinion networks when a controller must choose both a small set of agents and a scalar intervention at each update. We use the affine free response, which includes DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen dynamics, followed by a direct sparse action. Eliminating the scalar action reduces the one-step problem to a cardinality-constrained support objective whose global optimum is obtained from the largest or smallest residual components. Hence exact sparse one-step-ahead optimal control requires one sort and \(O(|\mathcal T|\log|\mathcal T|)\) operations, where \(\mathcal T\) is the support set.
  For periodic affine dynamics, the resulting state-dependent low-rank feedback admits cycle-based ultimate bounds and exact tracking of model-invariant targets. The DeGroot and Friedkin--Johnsen specializations expose the role of target invariance and the affine mismatch term. With several competing external players, each sparse best response retains the same sorting structure and the complete binary--continuous stage game is an exact potential game, so a pure-strategy equilibrium exists at every frozen state. Same-state welfare benchmarks separate loss due to scalar competition from the additional loss due to strategic support selection. Numerical examples validate exact support selection and illustrate switching-order effects, DeGroot/Friedkin--Johnsen tracking, resource tradeoffs, and competitive implementations.

</details>


### [314] [Advances in Modeling Techniques for Ventricular Assist Devices: A Comprehensive Review and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20518)
*Muhammad Adel Yusuf,Nezar M. Alyazidi,Hamna Saleem,Ali Nasir,Mojeed Oyedeji*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 这篇综述系统性回顾了100余项LVAD建模与控制研究，指出LVAD控制临床转化受限的根源不在算法复杂度不足，而在于建模假设、传感局限与真实心血管变异之间的错配，并提出了问题驱动的分析框架与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转式左心室辅助装置（LVAD）对无法移植的晚期心衰患者至关重要，尽管心血管建模与控制研究已有进展，但所提出的控制方法在临床转化上依然有限。作者旨在解释这一转化鸿沟的真正原因，并梳理相关研究范式。

Method: 采用系统化综述方法，回顾超过100篇同行评审研究，涵盖数学心血管模型、集总参数与降阶表示、经典与先进控制策略、以及新兴的数据驱动与机器学习方法；并用问题驱动框架将建模与控制选择同临床挑战（生理可观测性、参数可辨识性、患者变异性、心室抽吸与血栓等不良事件预防）联系起来进行综合。

Result: 分析表明，高保真模型与智能控制器在仿真中表现良好，但其对不可测状态的依赖、大量参数整定和密集传感需求限制了临床实施；在临床约束下，较简单的控制方法往往具有更强的鲁棒性。自适应与数据驱动技术有望弥合差距，但前提是必须考虑植入式传感局限性与可解释性要求。

Conclusion: 通过识别阻碍临床采纳的结构性障碍，本文综合了LVAD建模与控制范式，并勾勒出面向生理自适应、临床可实施且患者安全的LVAD系统的研究方向。

Abstract: Ventricular Assist Devices (VADs), particularly rotary Left Ventricular Assist Devices (LVADs), are essential for patients with advanced heart failure who are ineligible for transplantation. Despite advances in cardiovascular modeling and control, clinical translation of proposed LVAD control methods remains limited. This review examines the evolution of LVAD modeling and control and argues that the gap results not from insufficiently sophisticated algorithms, but from mismatches between modeling assumptions, sensing limitations, and real-world cardiovascular variability.
  A systematized review of more than 100 peer-reviewed studies covers mathematical cardiovascular models, lumped-parameter and reduced-order representations, classical and advanced control strategies, and emerging data-driven and machine learning approaches. The literature is synthesized using a problem-driven framework linking modeling and control choices to clinical challenges, including physiological observability, parameter identifiability, patient variability, and prevention of adverse events such as ventricular suction and thrombosis.
  The analysis shows that high-fidelity models and intelligent controllers can perform well in simulation, but their dependence on unmeasurable states, extensive parameter tuning, and dense sensing limits clinical implementation. Simpler control approaches often provide greater robustness under clinical constraints. Adaptive and data-driven techniques may help bridge this gap, but only if they account for implantable sensing limitations and interpretability requirements.
  By identifying structural barriers to adoption, this review synthesizes LVAD modeling and control paradigms and outlines research directions for physiologically adaptive, clinically implementable, and patient-safe LVAD systems.

</details>


### [315] [Large Language Models as Falsifiers for Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20752)
*Ali ArjomandBigdeli,Jiawei Zhou,Stanley Bak*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出LLM-Falsifier，用大语言模型通过最小化STL鲁棒度来对CPS规范进行反例搜索，并引入语义信息提升搜索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS规范伪造传统上用黑盒搜索算法求解鲁棒度优化问题，而LLM结合迭代提示已展现出优化能力，但标准数值优化器缺乏语言模型可利用的语义信息。

Method: 将伪造问题建模为STL鲁棒度最小化，通过迭代提示引导LLM优化；关键是为LLM提供自然语言输入/输出名称、输出轨迹以及最小鲁棒度对应的关键时刻见证等语义信息。

Result: 在ARCH-COMP伪造基准上，以找到反例所需的平均模拟次数衡量，LLM-Falsifier在21个规范中的14个上优于基于代理模型、贝叶斯优化和基于搜索的测试等现有伪造工具。

Conclusion: 将LLM作为优化器并注入语义信息，可显著提升CPS规范伪造的样本效率，优于传统优化范式。

Abstract: Falsification searches for counterexamples to formal specifications in cyber-physical systems (CPS). With specifications written in Signal Temporal Logic (STL), falsification can be formulated as a robustness optimization problem, traditionally tackled with black-box search algorithms. In parallel, large language models (LLMs) have recently emerged as surprisingly effective optimizers when coupled with iterative prompting. In this work, we connect these ideas and introduce LLM-Falsifier, an LLM-based approach that falsifies specifications by minimizing the STL robustness degree. Beyond generic prompt-based optimization, our key idea is to expose the LLM to semantic information that is natural for language models but absent from standard numerical optimizers, including natural-language input and output names, output trajectories, and critical-time witnesses for the minimum robustness value. These additions enable smarter and more sample-efficient robustness search. On the ARCH-COMP falsification benchmarks, LLM-Falsifier is shown to outperform existing falsification tools based on a range of optimization paradigms, from surrogate-based and Bayesian optimization to search-based testing, on 14 of 21 specifications when measured by the average number of simulations required to find a counterexample.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [316] [Federated Soft Clustering via Generalized Total Variation Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19202)
*Shamsiiat Abdurakhmanova,Alexander Jung*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究联邦软聚类，比较GTVMin中三种差异度量（欧氏距离、KL散度、MMD）用于个性化GMM，用同步投影梯度优化并评估鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，各设备持有私有数据并训练个性化高斯混合模型，需要一种图谱正则化（GTVMin）来耦合本地最大似然问题；而差异度量方式的选择是关键设计决策，直接影响是否需要组件匹配及模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 比较三种差异度量：参数间的平方欧氏距离（需组件匹配）、蒙特卡洛近似的KL散度、闭式MMD（后两者直接比较本地模型分布，无需匹配）。三种GTVMin实例均用同步投影梯度更新优化，对光滑的MMD实例给出收敛到稳定点的保证，并刻画计算成本。

Result: 刻画了三种方法的计算成本，并评估了它们对数据异质性的鲁棒性；MMD实例具有收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 不同差异度量在是否需要组件匹配、计算成本和鲁棒性上各有取舍，MMD在提供收敛保证的同时比较分布更直接。

Abstract: We study federated soft clustering over federated learning (FL) networks of devices that each hold a private local dataset and fit a personalized Gaussian mixture model (GMM). Generalized total variation minimization (GTVMin) couples the local maximum likelihood problems through a graph regularizer that penalizes a discrepancy between the models of connected nodes. The choice of discrepancy measure is a key design decision: we compare a squared Euclidean distance between model parameters, which requires component matching, with two measures that compare the local model distributions directly and hence need no matching: a Monte-Carlo approximated Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and a closed-form maximum mean discrepancy (MMD). All three resulting GTVMin instances are optimized by synchronous projected gradient updates; for the smooth MMD instance we provide a convergence guarantee to stationary points. We characterize their computational cost and evaluate their robustness to data heterogeneity.

</details>


### [317] [Learning Submanifolds for Subsequent Inference on Random Dot Product Graphs, Part 1: Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19357)
*Michael W. Trosset,Carey E. Priebe*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该文提出一个针对随机点积图（RDPG）在未知低维支撑流形上的受限推断框架，利用辅助数据学习支撑流形，并证明半监督决策规则的渐近最优性（收敛到oracle规则）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机点积图的潜在位置可能位于未知的低维支撑流形上，传统的推断方法未充分利用这种低维结构；作者希望借助辅助数据学习该流形，从而在受限条件下做出更优决策。

Method: 使用Isomap流形学习构造图的低维欧氏表示，在该空间中用等距不变函数将点的构型映射为动作，从而构造半监督决策规则。

Result: 随着辅助样本量增加，半监督规则的风险收敛到利用支撑流形可提取的最大低维欧氏结构的oracle规则的风险。

Conclusion: 半监督决策规则在辅助数据充足时渐近达到oracle性能；示例、应用和仿真研究将在后续论文中给出。

Abstract: We propose a framework for restricted inference on random dot product graphs whose latent positions lie on an unknown low-dimensional support manifold. For general decision problems, we propose semisupervised decision rules that use auxiliary data to learn the support manifold. Specifically, our rules use the Isomap manifold learning procedure to construct a low-dimensional Euclidean representation of the observed graph, in which space an isometrically invariant function maps configurations of points to actions. We study the behavior of the proposed rules as the quantity of auxiliary data sampled from the unknown support manifold increases. We show that, as the auxiliary sample size increases, the risk of the semisupervised rule converges to the risk of an oracle rule that relies on the maximal amount of low-dimensional Euclidean structure that can be extracted from the support manifold. Examples, applications, and simulation studies are deferred to a sequel.

</details>


### [318] [Null importance: Disentangling relevance for interpretable machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19511)
*Garvesh Raskutti,Kris Sankaran,Jiaxin Ye*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"零重要性"（null importance）的统一框架，刻画不同相关性子概念下的特征无关性，梳理其统计、风险、不变性与因果来源，并连接科学问题、数据假设与重要性评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特征重要性是解释性机器学习的核心，但"重要性"包含若干根本不同的相关性概念，现有方法常在混用这些概念，导致结论含糊甚至误导。

Method: 以总体水平的零重要性定义为统一视角，区分边际/条件统计相关性、预测风险、函数不变性和因果效应等多种零重要性概念；建立它们重合的充分条件并给出反例；刻画不同方法族所针对的零概念及其零重要性统计量何时能识别目标；辅以覆盖特征依赖、冗余、非线性、隐藏特征等的模拟及图像与多组学案例研究。

Result: 证明各类零重要性在特定条件下重合，否则会分歧；说明各方法族实际针对的零概念及其识别条件；在算法公平性（不同公平准则对应不同零重要性）与基因组扰动建模中展示了概念区分带来的实质后果；模拟与案例提供实证支持。

Conclusion: 该框架为科学问题、数据生成假设与算法提供共同统计语言，明确了特征重要性分析所能支持的结论及其边界。

Abstract: Feature importance is central to interpretable machine learning, but the term "importance" encompasses several fundamentally different notions of relevance. We develop a unified perspective based on null importance: a population-level characterization of when a feature is irrelevant under a specified notion of relevance. We consider standard notions of null importance arising from marginal and conditional statistical relevance, predictive risk, functional invariance, and causal effects, and show how these notions answer different scientific questions. We illustrate the framework in two applications in which the distinction is particularly consequential: algorithmic fairness, where common fairness criteria correspond to different notions of null importance, and genomic perturbation modeling, where different notions of relevance lead to different conclusions about what a prediction model has learned. The framework connects three aspects of feature analysis: the scientific question defining relevance, the data and model assumptions that shape how different null notions relate, and the methods used to assess importance. We establish sufficient conditions under which null notions coincide and give counterexamples showing how they diverge when those conditions fail. We then characterize which nulls different method families target and when their zero-importance statistics identify those targets. Finally, simulations spanning feature dependence, redundancy, nonlinearity, hidden features and other standard phenomena, along with case studies on image and multiomics data, provide empirical evidence for these theoretical distinctions and their practical consequences. Taken together, these results provide a common statistical language for relating scientific questions, data-generating assumptions, and algorithms, and clarify the conclusions that feature-importance analyses can support.

</details>


### [319] [Next-token functional estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19529)
*Milind Nakul,Vidya Muthukumar,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出leave-a-window-out估计量，用于估计依赖序列中next-token功能量，理论上证明其在平稳β-mixing过程达到参数收敛率，并在模拟中优于leave-one-out基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典leave-one-out方法在时间依赖下不一致，无法准确估计next-token功能量（如surprise probability、tail probability、分类器测试误差）。

Method: 提出leave-a-window-out估计：在每个索引后删除长度τ的窗口再构造经验测度，τ=1时退化为leave-one-out；在平稳β-mixing且具Marton耦合的假设下分析。

Result: 理论证明估计误差按参数速率衰减，覆盖多类随机过程；并给出mixing Markov链上surprise probability的sharp minimax下界。模拟在Markov链、移动平均、自回归过程中成功，而leave-one-out与add-constant基线失效。

Conclusion: leave-a-window-out是处理时间依赖下next-token功能量估计的有效方法，兼具参数收敛率与更优的实际表现。

Abstract: Suppose we observe the first $n$ points of a sequence of random variables having length $n+1$, and wish to estimate a functional of the unobserved final point and the empirical measure of the $n$ observed training points. Such next-token functionals include the probability that the next token is novel (also known as the surprise probability), the tail probability of the minimum distance between the next token and training points, and the test error of a classifier trained on the observed points. All of these quantities are classically estimated by the leave-one-out method, which is inconsistent under temporal dependence. We propose a leave-a-window-out estimator, which deletes a window of length $τ$ after each index before forming the empirical measure and reduces to leave-one-out at $τ= 1$. Under natural assumptions, we show that the error of our estimator decays at a parametric rate for any stationary $β$-mixing process that also admits a Marton coupling. Our results thus cover several natural functionals on a large class of stochastic processes. We complement these upper bounds with a sharp minimax lower bound for estimating the surprise probability on mixing Markov chains. Simulations on Markov chains, moving-average processes, and autoregressive processes show that our estimator succeeds in many scenarios where leave-one-out and add-constant baselines fail.

</details>


### [320] [Error bounds in Sobolev norms for approximations with norm constrained ReLU neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19937)
*Xianjun Li,Yunfei Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将ReLU网络的路径范数约束逼近从一致逼近推广到Sobolev范数逼近，分别给出浅层和深层网络的误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究证明ReLU网络在权重路径范数约束下能很好逼近光滑函数，但这些结果限于一致逼近，本文希望将其扩展到Sobolev范数逼近。

Method: 分析受路径范数K约束、宽度W、深度L的神经网络对$W^{n,p}$中Sobolev函数的逼近能力，误差以$W^{1,p}$范数度量；分别考察浅层（L=1）和深层网络。

Result: 浅层网络（L=1）在$n<s=(d+3)/2$时误差界为$\mathcal{O}(\max\{W^{-(n-1)/d}, K^{-(n-1)/(s-n)}\})$；深层网络在宽度和深度足够大时，误差界为$\mathcal{O}(K^{-(n-1)/(d+d/p+1)})$，且去除了光滑性限制。

Conclusion: 深层网络能够去除光滑性指标限制，实现对Sobolev函数的有效逼近，扩展了路径范数约束下的逼近理论。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that smooth functions can be well approximated by ReLU neural networks with path norm constraint on the weights. We extend these results from uniform approximation to approximation in Sobolev norm. Specifically, we analyze how well Sobolev functions in $W^{n,p}$ can be approximated by neural networks with width $W$, depth $L$ and path norm bounded by $K$, when the approximation error is measured in the $W^{1,p}$-norm. For shallow networks with depth $L=1$, we derive the approximation error bound $\mathcal{O}(\max\{W^{-(n-1)/d}, K^{-(n-1)/(s-n)}\})$, when the smoothness index satisfies $n<s=(d+3)/2$ and the input is $d$-dimensional. For deep networks, we remove the restriction on the smoothness by showing that the approximation bound $\mathcal{O}(K^{-(n-1)/(d+d/p+1)})$ holds if the width $W$ and depth $L$ are sufficiently large.

</details>


### [321] [Model-based Bootstrap for Offline Policy Evaluation in Tabular Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20389)
*Weiwei Wang,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu,Bingyi Jing*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型的自举(bootstrap)框架，用于有限时域、时间非齐次MDP中离线策略评估(OPE)的不确定性量化，能统一处理多种离线数据格式并提升有限样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险强化学习应用中，部署新策略前需可靠评估，仅有点估计不足，需要置信区间和方差估计等原则性不确定性量化。估算评估误差的抽样分布可统一这些任务，但现有方法在鲁棒性、可扩展性和有限样本有效性上存在局限。

Method: 提出基于模型的自举框架：不同于依赖重采样完整episode的经典bootstrap，该方法从估计的MDP中重新生成轨迹，从而支持更广泛的离线数据格式（完整轨迹、转移级观测、轨迹片段），并提升有限样本统计效率。

Result: 建立了自举分布的相合性、渐近有效的置信区间，以及目标策略值的相合方差估计。大量仿真表明该方法能准确刻画OPE估计量的抽样分布，在多数设置下给出更紧的置信区间和更准确的方差估计。

Conclusion: 该基于模型的自举框架为OPE提供了有效的不确定性量化，具备良好的灵活性、统计有效性和实用性能。

Abstract: Offline policy evaluation (OPE) is crucial in high-stakes reinforcement learning applications, where new policies must be assessed reliably before deployment. In such settings, point estimates alone are insufficient; principled uncertainty quantification, such as confidence intervals and variance estimates, is essential for safe and risk-aware decision-making. A comprehensive way to unify these tasks is to estimate the sampling distribution of the evaluation error. Existing approaches, however, often suffer from limited robustness, scalability, or finite-sample validity. In this paper, we propose a model-based bootstrap framework for uncertainty quantification of OPE in finite-horizon, time-inhomogeneous Markov decision processes (MDPs). Unlike classical bootstrap methods that rely on resampling complete episodes, the proposed method regenerates trajectories from an estimated MDP and can therefore accommodate a much broader range of offline data formats, including complete trajectories, transition-level observations, and trajectory fragments. This flexibility further improves finite-sample statistical efficiency. We establish bootstrap distributional consistency, asymptotically valid confidence intervals, and consistent variance estimation for the target policy value. Extensive simulations show that the proposed method accurately captures the sampling distribution of the OPE estimator, yielding tighter confidence intervals and more accurate variance estimates in most settings.

</details>


### [322] [Online Supervised Dimension Reduction with Random Features: Diagnostics and Computational Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20454)
*Zhenlin Yao,Wei Xiong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究OKSPCA中监督目标优化精度、总体子空间恢复与预测表示质量之间的区别，通过一致性/集中/扰动分析与六项基准实验表明：目标值优化好不等于子空间或预测更优，且数值成本与质量需分开评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确优化监督谱目标并不必然带来准确的总体子空间或更好的预测表示，作者希望厘清这些常被混淆的概念，并以OKSPCA为对象检验该区分。

Method: 针对OKSPCA（有限随机特征坐标下的中心交叉矩 + Adam式正交基更新），给出固定映射一致性、集中与扰动结果来描述估计量及其精确子空间；再用同目标比较单独评估实际迭代，并在六个预测基准和数值服务负载上做实验。

Result: 替换追踪器为精确经验目标后，两个回归缺陷基本不变；分类秩模型平均捕获几乎全部终端目标能量，但保存的中间状态存在明显几何偏差；样本量研究进一步分离经验精度与总体恢复；数值服务中按需精确计算在某些分类设置更快，Adam相对完整thin-SVD服务在部分稠密宽回归请求上省时但仍持续存在几何误差。

Conclusion: 这些诊断限制了仅用终端优化精度来解释性能的做法，明确区分数值成本与质量、秩覆盖和新鲜度，既不证明实际追踪器收敛，也不证明基可用性带来预测或部署收益。

Abstract: Accurate optimization of a supervised spectral objective need not produce an accurate population subspace or a better predictive representation. We investigate these distinctions for Online Kernel Supervised Principal Component Analysis (OKSPCA), which combines a centered cross-moment in finite random-feature coordinates with an Adam-style orthonormal basis update for an established objective. Fixed-map consistency, concentration and perturbation results describe the estimator and its exact subspace; same-target comparisons then assess the practical iterate separately. Across six predictive benchmarks, performance depends on the declared pipeline: replacing the tracker with the exact empirical target leaves the two regression deficits largely unchanged. Direct classification-rank models capture nearly all terminal objective energy on average, but a saved intermediate state exhibits substantial geometric deviation; a controlled sample-size study further separates empirical accuracy from population recovery. In distinct numerical-service workloads, exact on-request computation is faster in the tested classification settings, whereas Adam saves time relative to the tested full thin-SVD service for some dense wider-regression requests, alongside persistent geometric error. These diagnostics limit explanations based solely on terminal optimization accuracy and distinguish numerical cost from quality, rank coverage and freshness; they establish neither practical-tracker convergence nor predictive or deployment benefits from basis availability.

</details>


### [323] [TAP Accuracy Below the Fluctuation Scale and Universal Posterior Geometry in Spherical Linear Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20577)
*Jingbo Liu,Zhiyuan Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究高维比例渐近下球面线性模型的Bayes最优性，在定量Marchenko-Pastur谱正则条件下证明全温度TAP近似，并刻画后验几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维比例渐近下理解球面线性模型的Bayes最优行为，并放宽以往对设计矩阵的独立性或奇异向量条件限制。

Method: 在设计的定量Marchenko-Pastur谱正则条件下（允许有限四阶矩的标准化i.i.d.设计，无需逐元素独立或奇异向量条件），证明定量全温度TAP近似并分析后验几何。

Result: 规范化球面自由能与TAP最优值相差O_P(p^{-1})，二者与显式确定性等价物相差O_P(p^{-1/2})且该波动阶是紧的；所有全局TAP极大点到球面后验均值的归一化平方欧氏距离为O_P(p^{-1})；由ridge估计量决定的数据依赖带外后验质量具有尖锐指数阶，对数质量至多-cpε²+O_P(1)，并给出匹配下界与带宽ε_p≫p^{-1/2}时捕获全部后验质量的结果。

Conclusion: 在较弱的谱正则条件下建立了高维球面线性模型的定量TAP理论与后验集中几何，波动尺度与指数阶均为尖锐。

Abstract: We study the Bayes-optimal spherical linear model as the ambient dimension and sample size grow proportionally, under a quantitative Marchenko--Pastur spectral-regularity condition on the design. This condition is satisfied by normalized i.i.d. designs with standardized entries of finite fourth moment, but does not require entrywise independence or impose conditions on the singular vectors. Under this condition, we prove a quantitative all-temperature TAP approximation and characterize the posterior geometry. For the natural finite-aspect-ratio TAP functional, the normalized spherical free energy and the TAP optimum differ by $O_P(p^{-1})$. Each is within $O_P(p^{-1/2})$ of its explicit deterministic equivalent, and this fluctuation scale is sharp. Uniformly over all global TAP maximizers, the normalized squared Euclidean distance to the spherical posterior mean is $O_P(p^{-1})$. We also prove that the posterior mass outside a data-dependent band determined by the ridge estimator has sharp exponential order. More precisely, uniformly over sufficiently small band widths $\varepsilon$, the logarithm of this mass is at most $-cp\varepsilon^2+O_P(1)$. For every fixed geometrically admissible width, a spherical-cap construction gives a matching exponential-order lower bound on this mass. For every deterministic sequence of widths $\varepsilon_p\gg p^{-1/2}$, the corresponding bands capture asymptotically all posterior mass.

</details>


### [324] [Prediction-Powered Smoothing and Validation for Disaggregated AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20758)
*Sho Kawano,Zehang Richard Li,Paul A. Parker*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出预测驱动平滑(PP-S)及其跨分类借力扩展(PP-TS),并给出近似无偏的设计型交叉验证分数,用于AI系统分域评估的点估计与区间估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统评估需按域(如任务类型、对话类型)分别评估,但穷尽测试代价高昂,只能依赖样本。直接估计器(含PPI)仅用本域标签,标签少时估计不精确,尤其在小域上区间估计与覆盖性差。

Method: 将评估集视为有限总体,借助小域估计(small area estimation)思想构建估计与验证的整合流程。估计方面,对每个域的PPI估计拟合贝叶斯模型得到PP-S;并通过报告分类体系跨域借力得到PP-TS。验证方面,推导出新的、近似无偏的设计型交叉验证分数,用于在直接估计器与平滑估计器之间选择。

Result: 在两个数据集上验证:一个可自动评分的人工构建基准,以及由人工评分的已部署智能体流量(两者所有结果均可观测)。所提估计器在点估计与区间估计上均优于直接估计器,覆盖率接近名义水平;在相同采样预算下,该选择分数与独立验证样本选择效果相当,且对所选估计器的误差估计更准确。

Conclusion: 将小域估计与预测驱动推断结合,可有效提升AI分域评估的精度与区间可靠性;设计型交叉验证分数为估计器选择提供了低成本、近乎无偏的方案,降低了对额外标注样本的需求。

Abstract: Evaluating an AI system requires disaggregated assessment, as performance varies across domains such as benchmark task types or conversation types in deployed agents. Exhaustive testing is expensive, so evaluation rests on a sample of labeled units. We treat the evaluation set as a finite population and seek accurate point and interval estimates of each domain mean. Direct estimators, including prediction-powered inference (PPI), use only a domain's own labels and are imprecise where labels are few. Small area estimation addresses this problem, and we build on it to develop an integrated workflow for estimation and validation. For estimation, we propose prediction-powered smoothing (PP-S), a Bayesian model fit to each domain's prediction-powered estimate, with an extension that borrows strength across a reporting taxonomy (PP-TS). For validation, we derive a new, approximately unbiased design-based cross-validation score for choosing among direct and smoothed estimators. We study a curated benchmark with verifiable grading and deployed agent traffic graded by humans, each with every outcome observed. In both, the proposed estimators improve on the direct estimators in point and interval estimation, with near-nominal coverage. At the same sampling budget, our score selects as well as an independent validation sample does and estimates the selected estimator's error far more accurately.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [Making Local Government Contracts Legible: A Computational Pipeline for Classifying and Mapping Intergovernmental Service Agreements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19225)
*Mohsen Ghasemizade,Raúl Gutiérrez-Meave,Cailin Gramling,Aviral Chawla,Michael Robinette,Kate Albrecht,Juniper Lovato*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端计算流程，用LLM对政府间协议按制度形式分类并抽取财务关系，应用于爱荷华州28E档案（N=21,629），构建有向财务网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地方政府间协议是公共服务协作的主要工具，但合同中的制度与财务内容规模庞大、难以系统分析。

Method: 结合LLaMA 3.1、GPT 5.2 Pro、Gemini 3 Pro进行摘要与四类分类（服务合同、资源共享、联合运营、新联合实体），识别财务委托方与代理方及金额，构建有向网络。

Result: 财务网络围绕少数主导服务提供者组织；县是最具结构多样性的主体，城市主要作为委托方。

Conclusion: 该流程首次使爱荷华州政府间协议内容可规模化分析，为研究者与州机构追踪公共资金流向、识别依赖少数提供者的实体提供可复用方法。

Abstract: Interlocal agreements are one of the primary instruments through which local governments formalize collaboration for public service delivery, yet the institutional and financial content encoded in these contracts has remained inaccessible to systematic analysis at scale. This paper introduces an end-to-end computational pipeline for classifying intergovernmental agreements by institutional form and extracting financial relationships between principals and agents in service contracts. Applied to Iowa's 28E archive (N = 21,629), the largest dataset of interlocal agreements in the United States, the pipeline combines LLM-based summarization and classification across LLaMA 3.1, GPT 5.2 Pro, and Gemini 3 Pro on a four-class classification task that distinguishes agreements as either service contracts, resource sharing agreements, joint operations agreements, or new joint entity agreements. We also identify the financial principal and agent in these agreements and contracts, as well as the resulting dollar amounts and represent them on a directed network. The resulting financial network is organized around a small number of dominant service providers, with counties serving as the most structurally versatile actors, and cities as predominantly principals. By rendering the content of Iowa interlocal agreements analyzable at scale for the first time, this pipeline establishes a reusable methodology that researchers and state agencies can apply to track how public dollars move across local governments and to identify entities that depend heavily on a small number of providers.

</details>


### [326] [The AR Fairness Metamodel: A Structured Framework for Fairness Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19234)
*Julian Alfredo Mendez,Timotheus Kampik*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出AR公平元模型，用Tiles框架统一表示、分析和比较多种公平场景与度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同公平定义（平等、公平、群体/个体公平、各类指数）分散且难以统一定义和比较，缺乏可跨场景适配的实用框架。

Method: 构建AR公平元模型，以智能体、资源及其属性为核心要素；基于Tiles概念建模框架提供模块化组件，可组合定义多种公平度量，并给出形式化证明探讨群体公平、个体公平与无嫉妒之间的关系。

Result: 给出离散与连续度量的示例，涵盖平等、公平、群体/个体公平、Gini、Theil、Jain指数及澳大利亚儿童保育补贴公平度量；形式化证明了相关公平概念间的关系；Tiles框架以开源工具实现。

Conclusion: 该元模型使AR类公平定义更实用、可适配，提供清晰的定义、比较与评估方式，并借助开源工具支持广泛应用中的公平建模与评估。

Abstract: This paper presents the AR fairness metamodel, a framework designed to represent, analyze, and compare different fairness scenarios. The metamodel considers key elements, such as agents, resources, and their attributes, and enables the systematic definition and comparison of various fairness measures. We provide examples involving both discrete and continuous measures, including equality, equity, group fairness, individual fairness, the Gini index, the Theil index, Jain's fairness index, and a detailed fairness measure for Australia's Child Care Subsidy. We also explore relationships among group fairness, individual fairness, and envy-freeness, supported by formal proofs. At the conceptual modeling level, our approach builds on the Tiles framework, which offers modular components that can be connected to capture diverse fairness definitions. The goal is to make AR-based fairness definitions practical and adaptable across contexts, providing a clear way to define, compare, and evaluate them. An implementation of the Tiles framework is available as an open-source tool, and can support fairness modeling and evaluation across a wide range of applications.

</details>


### [327] [From Digital Competence to Demonstrated Digital Capability: Positioning the International Digital Driving License Against DigComp and UNESCO Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19406)
*Ahmad Ghandour*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文通过比较DigComp、UNESCO数字素养与AI能力框架及IDDL，指出能力框架定义"应知应会"，而IDDL通过真实任务中的行为观察评估"实际能做"，可作为下游可互操作的行为评估与认证层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式与代理式AI的发展提出新问题：知道何为有能力的数字行为，是否足以证明个人能在真实数字情境中胜任行动？现有框架多定义知识、技能、态度与价值观，缺少对实际行为能力的证据性评估。

Method: 概念性论文，采用比较分析，对DigComp、UNESCO数字素养全球框架、UNESCO学生AI能力框架与IDDL进行功能区分而非竞争性比较。

Result: DigComp与UNESCO主要界定"应知、应理解、应重视、应能做"；IDDL则通过真实数字任务中的行为观察评估数字能力，其"知识—能力—反思"模型区分知道该做什么、展示所需行为、解释判断依据，并整合数字技能、网络安全意识与AI能力，将评估延伸至AI委托情境下的人类能动性。

Conclusion: 论文区分了"已定义的胜任力"与"已展示的实际能力"，将IDDL定位为可互操作的行为评估与认证层，可运行于国际能力框架的下游。

Abstract: Digital competence frameworks define the knowledge, skills, attitudes and values required for participation in digital society. The European Digital Competence Framework for Citizens (DigComp) and UNESCO digital literacy and artificial intelligence competency frameworks provide reference structures for policy, curriculum and competency development. The growth of generative and agentic AI raises a further question: does knowing what constitutes competent digital behaviour provide sufficient evidence that an individual can act competently in an authentic digital situation? This conceptual paper addresses that question through a comparative analysis of DigComp, UNESCO Digital Literacy Global Framework, UNESCO AI Competency Framework for Students, and the International Digital Driving License (IDDL). Rather than treating these frameworks as competing models, the paper distinguishes their functions. DigComp and UNESCO primarily define what individuals should know, understand, value and be able to do. IDDL addresses the subsequent evidentiary problem by assessing digital capability through behaviour observed during authentic digital tasks. Its Knowledge-Capability-Reflection model distinguishes knowing what should be done, demonstrating the required behaviour, and explaining the judgement behind it. IDDL integrates Digital Skills, Cybersecurity Awareness and AI Competency, and extends assessment to human agency under AI delegation. The paper distinguishes defined competence from demonstrated capability and positions IDDL as an interoperable behavioural assessment and certification layer that can operate downstream of international competency frameworks.

</details>


### [328] [BurnRiSc: Toward Non-Invasive Burnout Screening in Open Source from Public Repository Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19422)
*Timofey Sanko,Yuan Tian,Mariam Guizani*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出 BurnRiSc，将 Oldenburg 倦怠量表的"耗竭"与"疏离"两个维度操作化为 14 个基于 GitHub 活动的行为/语言信号，并按贡献者自身历史打分，聚合为月度倦怠风险分（BRS）。在 68 名贡献者上的初步评估显示，持续 BRS 升高可提前 6–15 个月预警 10 例公开倦怠中的 6 例（加峰值 BRS 则 8 例，任意时间窗下 10 例），证明倦怠可从公开数据筛查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源维护是倦怠的"最坏情形"：需求无上限、无管理者调配工作、无组织察觉衰退。倦怠先于退出，而少数维护者的离职可导致数千下游系统依赖的基础设施崩溃。然而现有唯一测量手段——自我报告量表——恰恰漏掉了最需要被检测的贡献者，且无法回溯，导致领域连"倦怠有多普遍、什么方法有效"都无法回答。

Method: BurnRiSc 将 Oldenburg 倦怠量表的耗竭与疏离两维度操作化为 14 个行为与语言信号，从 GitHub 活动计算，并以每位贡献者自身历史为基线进行打分。信号聚合为两个加权维度分数，权重由标注案例学习得到，再平均为月度倦怠风险分（BRS）。

Result: 在 10 个仓库共 68 名贡献者上的初步评估中（10 例公开倦怠、12 例可比的活跃度骤降、46 名对照），持续 BRS 升高提前 6–15 个月预判 10 例披露中的 6 例；加入峰值 BRS 作为第二标准后达 8/10；在任何先前时间窗下达到 10/10。

Conclusion: BurnRiSc 表明倦怠可仅凭公开数据被筛查出来，为开源领域提供了可回溯、覆盖自我报告缺失人群的倦怠风险检测途径。

Abstract: Burnout is a chronic occupational syndrome, and open source is close to a worst case for it: maintainers absorb unbounded demand with no manager to reallocate work and no organization to notice decline. The cost is not only personal. Burnout precedes withdrawal, and in projects sustained by a handful of maintainers, one departure can break infrastructure that thousands of downstream systems depend on. Yet the field has no way to see it coming: self-report inventories, the only existing measure, miss exactly the contributors most in need of detection and cannot be applied retroactively, so the field cannot even ask how common burnout is or what helps.
  We present BurnRiSc, a framework that operationalizes the Oldenburg Burnout Inventory's two dimensions, exhaustion and disengagement, as 14 behavioral and linguistic signals computed from GitHub activity and scored against each contributor's own history. The signals aggregate into two weighted dimension scores, with weights learned from labeled cases, and average into a monthly Burnout Risk Score (BRS). In a preliminary evaluation across 68 contributors in ten repositories (ten disclosed burnout cases, twelve comparable-volume collapses, and 46 comparison contributors), sustained BRS elevation precedes 6 of 10 disclosures by 6-15 months, 8 of 10 when adding peak BRS as a second criterion, and 10 of 10 over any prior time frame. We thus present BurnRiSc as evidence that burnout is screenable from public data.

</details>


### [329] [Looking Back and Forward: What Teaching Materials Do Not Remember About Instructional Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19488)
*Yi Ching Chou,Hao Fang,Haoyuan Zhao,Jiangchuan Liu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 提出"Teaching Memory"设计方法，将教学推理视为核心知识，在教师现有工作流中以轻量方式保存教学推理，以支持延续与复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教学材料可以传递给新教师，但设计背后的教学意图、情境信息与反思洞察等教学推理很少被保留。传统LMS工具只存储教学产物，而非推理过程。

Method: 提出Teaching Memory设计方法，并给出一个面向设计的框架，在教师既有工作流中轻量地保存教学推理。

Result: （摘要未提供具体实验结果）论文以设计框架形式呈现，旨在实现教学推理的延续与复用。

Conclusion: 通过将教学推理作为首要知识形式并轻量嵌入现有工作流，可弥补LMS在推理保存方面的不足，支持教学经验的传承与复用。

Abstract: Instructional materials can be passed along to new instructors, yet the instructional reasoning, such as instructional intentions, contextual information, and reflective insights behind their design, is rarely preserved. Traditional Learning Management System (LMS) tools are designed to store instructional artifacts rather than reasoning. To address this issue, we propose Teaching Memory, a design approach that treats instructional reasoning as a primary form of knowledge. We present a design-oriented framework for preserving instructional reasoning in a lightweight manner within instructors' existing workflows, enabling continuity and reuse.

</details>


### [330] [Funding the runners-up beats a golden ticket](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19552)
*Haining Wang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文用10,625篇被拒论文的后续引用百分位，检验"少数支持评分"（golden ticket）是否能替代小组均值排名选出被忽视的好工作，结论是否定的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Golden ticket让单个评审者推翻整个小组的决定，其评分依据是否合理缺乏实证检验，需要评估少数支持排名能否选出本会被忽视的高质量工作。

Method: 以2017—2024年某计算机会议10,625篇被拒投稿为样本，用后续引用百分位为结果。少数支持排名以调整小组均值与评审人数后最高两分数之差衡量，与小组均值排名、调整最高分排名、分数方差排名及彩票分配等在相同名额预算下比较，并做配对分析。

Result: 在10%预算下，小组均值排名所选论文的引用百分位比少数支持排名高7.75分（95% bootstrap区间4.35–11.09），其他时期与引用窗口差异更大；少数支持排名与彩票相差不超过±5个百分点。调整最高分排名与配对比较均未显示少数支持的引用优势，分数方差排名在配对比较中也优于少数支持排名。估计覆盖69.8%可核实引用的合格被拒论文。

Conclusion: 小组均值排名更应作为评估golden ticket能否识别被忽视工作的基准，少数支持排名缺乏引用维度的优势。

Abstract: A golden ticket allows one reviewer to advance a proposal that the rest of a panel would reject. We assess its score-based rationale by comparing minority-support ranking with other rules for allocating the same number of places. We do so on 10,625 rejected submissions to a computer-science conference between 2017 and 2024, using later citation percentiles as the outcome. Minority-support ranking measured the gap between the two highest scores after adjustment for the panel mean and reviewer count. At a budget of 10% of the year's accepted count, panel-mean ranking selected submissions with a mean citation percentile 7.75 points higher than minority-support ranking (95% bootstrap interval 4.35 to 11.09). The difference was larger in the other sample periods and citation windows examined. At that budget, minority-support ranking was equivalent to a lottery within a margin of plus or minus 5 percentile points. Two further analyses, adjusted highest-score ranking and a matched comparison, found no clear citation advantage from minority support once the panel mean was accounted for. Ranking on score variance also outperformed minority-support ranking in a paired comparison. Estimates cover the 69.8% of eligible rejections whose later citations could be verified. These comparisons identify panel-mean ranking as a benchmark for evaluating whether golden tickets select work that would otherwise be overlooked.

</details>


### [331] [Who Decides? Agency and Legitimacy in Digital Educational Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19939)
*Eriam Schaffter,Ahmed Bounekkar*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出用"能动性"和"合法性"两个维度来分类数字教育系统(DES)，并将样本映射到二维平面上，以揭示空白区域并探讨算法化调节的可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字教育系统种类繁多，承诺各异，从授予学位到快速进步，采用的技术手段也千差万别，亟需一个统一框架来理解这些系统在学习过程结构上的根本差异。

Method: 提出两个维度——能动性(决策留给学习者的比例)和合法性(制度化的认证/认可体系)，将其转化为指标，构建二维平面，并对一组精选的常见DES样本进行映射。

Result: 通过映射样本，识别出该二维平面上人口稀少的区域(空白区域)，揭示了现有DES在能动性和合法性组合上的分布不均。

Conclusion: 该分类框架有助于理解DES的结构差异，并为未来DES如何从学习利益出发、通过算法调节这两个维度提供了设想方向。

Abstract: Digital Educational Systems (DES) enable millions of individuals to learn or to acquire new skills. Their promises vary from one platform to another: some grant genuine degrees, others pledge rapid progress, drawing for this purpose on a range of techniques, from the recommendation of learning resources through complex models to a plain course textbook uploaded onto a learning management system. In this article, we argue that categorizing DES along two dimensions allows to map differences in the way these DES structurally conceptualise the learning process. These underlying dimensions are at once dimensions of the student model and mechanisms built in the DES's designs; we identify them as agency and legitimacy. Indeed, DES are, on the one hand, anchored in an institutionalised regime that grants them legitimacy and, in turn, legitimises the learning undertaken on these platforms; on the other hand, they make design choices as to the share of decision left to the learner in the learning process. We map a curated sample of common DES onto a plane composed of these two dimensions translated into indicators. This allows us to highlight underpopulated regions of the plane and to envisage how a DES might regulate these two dimensions algorithmically, in the interest of learning.

</details>


### [332] [Governance-as-Code: Translating EU AI Act Technical Requirements into Executable Compliance Pipelines for Generative AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20016)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出 Governance-as-Code (GaC) 框架，包含 43 条可机器校验的验收标准和六个合规模块，用于在 CI/CD 流程中自动生成附条文索引的合规证据，以弥补 EU AI Act 第 8-15 条在生成式 AI 上的七个技术缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EU AI Act 对高风险 AI 提供商设有技术义务，但第 8-15 条是为预测式 AI 起草的，应用于生成式系统时存在七个技术缺口（非确定性数据治理、训练数据溯源、持续符合性、人类监督、开放性鲁棒性、涌现风险、生成式公平性）；同时需厘清上游提供商与下游部署者之间的责任划分。

Method: 交付 GaC 框架：43 条机器可校验验收标准、六个合规模块，运行于 CI/CD 流水线并输出条文索引审计证据，展示实际 Rego 策略代码。将开放标准转化为可审计数值（鲁棒性阈值基于提供商基线加 SOTA 下限；框架偏见由反事实人口探测转为八个可测代理）；并按第 25 条与第五章说明下游部署者只需核验上游第 53 条训练数据摘要。

Result: 在高风险咨询聊天机器人和有限风险内容生成器两个企业部署中验证，并与人工专家审计对比（而非对比文档产物）。GaC 复现了人工审计的全部发现，包括三项触发处罚的违规，同时将审计人力减少约 75%。

Conclusion: GaC 证明可将 EU AI Act 的开放文本标准转化为声明式、可审计的机器校验标准，在 CI/CD 中自动化合规，既忠于法规责任分配，又显著提升审计效率，适用于生成式 AI 的合规实践。

Abstract: The EU AI Act (Regulation 2024/1689) imposes technical obligations on high-risk AI providers, yet Articles 8-15 were drafted for predictive AI and leave seven technical gaps when applied to generative systems, spanning non-deterministic data governance, training-data provenance, continuous conformity, human oversight, open-ended robustness, emergent risk, and generative fairness. We deliver Governance-as-Code (GaC), a framework of 43 machine-checkable acceptance criteria across six compliance modules that run in a CI/CD pipeline and emit Article-indexed audit evidence, and we show the actual Rego policy code rather than merely describing it. Our central commitment is that the Act's open-textured standards ("appropriate levels," "possible biases") become declared, auditable numbers: robustness thresholds are derived from the provider's documented baseline and a state-of-the-art floor, and framing bias is collapsed into eight measurable proxies tested by counterfactual demographic probing. We also correct who owes what, since under Article 25 and Chapter V a downstream deployer relies on the upstream provider's Article 53 training-data summary and documents only the layers it controls, so GaC verifies that summary rather than demanding per-sample documentation the deployer never had. We validate on two enterprise deployments, a high-risk advisory chatbot and a limited-risk content generator, benchmarking against a manual expert audit rather than documentation artifacts that were never designed to enforce compliance. GaC reproduces all of the manual audit's findings, including three penalty-triggering violations, while cutting audit labor by roughly 75%.

</details>


### [333] [Utilizing AI-Driven Project Management Tools for Optimized Talent Management in HRM: A Framework for Enhanced Resource Allocation and Performance Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20167)
*Jay Barach*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: TalentOptima是一款结合AI决策、预测分析与机器学习的HR管理工具，能自动化资源分配并与现有HR系统集成。通过40名管理者的模拟测试，证明其可降低HR成本、提升生产力、减少流失率与风险，被认为是优于其他HR框架的革新性工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业在人力资源管理上面临高要求任务，现有HR框架主要聚焦于"完成任务"，缺乏最优与创新的决策支持，因此需要一个能结合AI与预测分析、减轻人工负担并提升HR成效的工具。

Method: TalentOptima整合AI决策、先进预测分析和机器学习，实现自动化资源分配，并可无缝集成现有HR工具/框架。研究通过40名管理者参与的用户测试模拟，评估HR成本降低与工作生产力提升效果。

Result: 模拟结果显示：流失率下降，风险与资源管理表现改善，HR成本有望降低并提升生产力；相比其他HR框架，TalentOptima在最优与创新决策方面表现更优，被证明为"明显赢家"。

Conclusion: TalentOptima通过AI驱动的决策与预测能力，超越传统HR框架，长期为企业带来不可估量的价值，证明其具有革新性意义。

Abstract: When it comes to aiding businesses with demanding tasks regarding human resource management, TalentOptima unequivocally boasts of the best there is to offer. This tool utilizes AI based decision making, advanced predictive analytics, and also machine learning, all of which help in enabling automated resource allocation. To aid with better human resource management, TalentOptima integrates perfectly with already existing HR frameworks such as tools, etc. and shifts the focus towards aiding the user with insights while simultaneously alleviating manual work, this aids in a plethora of positive HR outcomes. A total of 40 managers participated in a simulation via user testing to ascertain if HR costs would reduce and work productivity would rise, the results were quite clear, attrition rates had dipped alongside risk and resource management rates, TalentOptima was a clear winner. Whereas the other HR frameworks primarily focused on ensuring work was done, TalentOptima ensured optimal and innovative decision-making, which overtime has proven to be invaluable for multiple companies, these results aid in proving why the tool is revolutionary.

</details>


### [334] [Detecting Deceptive Recruitment: A Signal-theoretic Machine Learning Framework for Early Identification of Labour Exploitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20336)
*Sajid Siraj,Mahnaz Hosseinzadeh,Amin Vafadarnikjoo,Shuyang Li*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文将网络虚假招聘广告识别形式化为信号理论下的分类问题，基于464个真实案例构建多模态检测模型，发现单模态已具较强判别力，文本质量与风险语言是关键指标，并开发了可解释的决策支持原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚假网络招聘广告是强迫劳动的主要入口，但因数据稀缺且缺乏经验验证指标，系统性检测方法仍不成熟。

Method: 在信号理论框架下，将检测定义为分类问题，认为剥削者发送模仿合法沟通的"无成本信号"，涉及文本、视觉和结构维度。收集9个来源国、21个行业、经反奴隶制慈善机构核实的464个案例（164虚假、300合法），结合计算机视觉、自然语言处理和语义嵌入构建多模态检测模型，并通过特征消融实验和重复分层交叉验证进行评估，使用SHAP进行解释分析。

Result: 各单一模态已达到较强判别力（ROC-AUC 0.87–0.97），多模态融合仅带来有限提升。SHAP分析显示文本质量和领域风险语言是主要判别特征，其中可读性指数、风险关键词密度和签证担保提及排名最高，其次是视觉颜色和纹理特征。

Conclusion: 这些生产质量差距反映了剥削者资源受限，无法在所有沟通渠道同时维持专业水准。作者将发现落地为提供可解释风险评分的概念验证决策支持系统，展示了严谨分析框架如何应对信息不对称和真实标签稀缺的人道主义运营难题。

Abstract: Deceptive online job advertisements have emerged as a primary pathway into forced labour, yet systematic detection methods remain underdeveloped due to data scarcity and absence of empirically validated indicators. We formalise this detection challenge as a classification problem under signalling theory, where exploiters transmit costless signals mimicking legitimate communications across textual, visual, and structural dimensions. Using 464 verified cases (164 deceptive, 300 legitimate) collected through anti-slavery charities across nine origin countries and 21 industries, we develop multimodal detection models combining computer vision, natural language processing, and semantic embeddings. Through systematic feature ablation experiments and repeated stratified cross-validation, we demonstrate that individual modalities achieve substantial discriminatory power (ROC-AUC: 0.87--0.97), whilst their integration yields modest further gains. SHAP-based analysis reveals that text quality and domain-specific risk language are the primary discriminators, with readability indices, risk keyword density, and visa sponsorship mentions ranking highest, followed by visual colour and texture features. These production quality gaps reflect resource constraints that prevent exploiters from maintaining professional standards across all communication channels simultaneously. We operationalise findings through a proof-of-concept decision support system providing interpretable risk scores for practitioners. This work demonstrates how rigorous analytical frameworks can address complex humanitarian operations challenges characterised by information asymmetry and limited ground-truth data.

</details>


### [335] [Welfare-Opaque Income: Taxation under AI-Agent Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20425)
*Yukun Zhang,Kemu Xu,Yishen Chen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究AI代理以隐藏规则执行经济决策时的所得税问题，提出"双重不可观测性"与"福利不透明"概念，并给出最优税制条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理的执行规则对政府不可见，同一可观测税基反应可能对应不同福利后果，传统税基统计不足以评估税改福利效应。

Method: 理论建模：在不可观测能力之外引入隐藏的"偏好—执行"映射，推导含响应加权执行楔子的最优税制充分统计条件，并给出楔子的识别与界；实验：4,500次模型运行、5个AI引擎的受控实验室对比。

Result: 税基统计可相同而改革福利效应不同，即使机械福利权重一致；高边际税率在局部过度执行下产生矫正收益、在执行不足下产生额外成本。实验中，忠实委托几乎所有运行都选得分最大化者；冲突目标下引擎反应异质（Claude基本保持，GLM总体下移，GPT-mini与Qwen集中在下尾上升，Qwen还有大幅下调）；显式分数使模型排序一致，公式化目标指令一致性较差；Qwen存在正向"税×目标"交互但方向不具跨引擎普适性，混合符号取决于是否纳入Qwen。

Conclusion: 执行信息应作为传统税基统计的补充；观测执行楔子可识别边际税改的福利效应，其界可转化为福利效应界。

Abstract: We study income taxation when an AI agent implements economically relevant choices through a rule hidden from the government. Alongside unobserved productive ability, this hidden preference-to-execution mapping creates \emph{double unobservability}: the same observable tax-base response can carry different welfare consequences. We call the resulting income \emph{welfare-opaque}. Our constructions show that tax-base statistics can coincide while reform welfare effects differ, even when mechanical welfare weights are identical. We derive an optimal-tax condition that adds a response-weighted execution wedge to the familiar sufficient statistics. A higher marginal rate gains a corrective benefit under local over-execution and an additional cost under local under-execution. Observing the wedge identifies the welfare effect of a marginal reform at the prevailing schedule; bounds on it deliver bounds on that effect.
  A controlled laboratory compares 4,500 model runs across five AI engines. Faithful delegation selects the score maximizer in essentially all runs. Conflicted objectives produce heterogeneous responses: Claude largely preserves the score maximizer, GLM moves predominantly downward, and GPT-mini and Qwen show concentrated lower-tail increases. Qwen also makes substantial downward adjustments. Different engines locate their departures at different points and in different directions of the designed distribution. Explicit scores align model rankings; formula-based objective instructions yield more uneven agreement. Qwen shows a clear positive tax-by-objective interaction, but its direction does not generalize across engines and the pooled sign depends on its inclusion. The analysis identifies execution information as a complement to conventional tax-base statistics.

</details>


### [336] [When Does the Public Become Suspicious of Bots? Demand-Side Evidence from Botometer Query Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20661)
*Tuğrulcan Elmas*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文利用Botometer查询日志作为"怀疑行为痕迹"，分析2020-2023年间超100万次公开机器人检查，揭示集体怀疑的触发因素、被查账号特征及公共反馈模式，将机器人检测查询日志确立为研究公众应对平台操纵的新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人检测研究多从供给侧或算法侧出发，缺乏对需求侧（用户何时怀疑、怀疑谁、结果如何）的理解。论文希望从公众私人性的"机器人检查"行为切入，考察其是否构成一种大众化、分布式的平台审计，从而开辟理解公众应对平台操纵的新途径。

Method: 以Botometer（最广泛使用的机器人检测服务）服务器端查询日志为数据来源，将每次查询视为怀疑行为痕迹；分析2020-2023年超100万次公开Twitter账号检查，并结合同期1%公共流中的32亿条推文进行丰富化分析。

Result: 集体怀疑随平台危机激增，在2022年Musk-Twitter机器人争议前后最为显著；被查账号更年长、更活跃、粉丝更多，多发布推广、政治和加密内容；引发集体怀疑的账号机器人分数更高、更易被封禁；Bot相关公众关注与Botometer活动在同期整体上升，但短期波动基本不耦合；公众反馈对人类多聚焦第一人称身份声明，对机器人和赛博格则引用发帖频率、政治内容、跨账号协调等证据性论证。

Conclusion: 机器人怀疑构成一种大众化、分布式的平台审计，能追踪自动化的有意义信号，表明审计工具查询日志可作为观察公众应对平台操纵行为的新颖透镜。

Abstract: We study private bot-checking behavior from the demand side: when people suspect an account is automated, whom they suspect, and what follows. Using Botometer's server-side query logs, the most widely used bot-detection service, we treat each query as a behavioural trace of suspicion. We analyze over 1 million public checks of Twitter accounts from 2020 to 2023, enriched with 3.2 billion tweets from the contemporaneous 1% public stream. Collective suspicion spikes with platform crises, most sharply around the 2022 Musk-Twitter bot dispute. Checked accounts are older and more prolific, have more followers, and post promotional, political, and crypto content. Accounts that draw collective suspicion have higher bot scores and are more likely to be suspended. Bot-related public attention and Botometer activity are elevated during the same broad periods, although their short-run fluctuations are largely uncoupled. Public feedback focuses on first-person identity claims for humans, and evidence-based arguments citing posting rate, political content, and cross-account coordination for bots and cyborgs. Bot suspicion thus constitutes a mass, distributed form of platform auditing that tracks meaningful signals of automation, establishing audit-tool query logs as a novel lens on public responses to platform manipulation.

</details>


### [337] [Harnessing Generative UI for Education: Tailored Learning Interactives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20738)
*Alisa Kovshov,Anisha Choudhury,Anna Iurchenko,Amy Keeling,Avinatan Hassidim,Ayelet Shasha Evron,Ayça Çakmakli,Diana Akrong,Femi Olanubi,Gal Elidan,Ian Li,Ido Lerer,Katherine Chou,Lidan Hackmon,Michal Gordon,Nir Kerem,Niv Efron,Preeti Singh,Rena Levitt,Rotem Yulzary,Shlomi Ben Shimon,Sophie Allweis,Tracey Lee-Joe,Tzvika Stein,Yaniv Carmel,Yael Haramaty,Yishay Mor,Yossi Matias,Yuri Lev*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该论文提出"learning interactives"框架，利用Gemini的生成式UI能力自动构建有效的定制化交互模拟，以解决传统交互式学习体验设计成本高、现成模型缺乏教学优化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式学习体验虽能提升学习效果，但设计费力、开发昂贵且需持续的教师指导。生成式UI虽可自动化创建定制交互体验，但现成模型缺乏对教学精准度和学习原则的优化，对复杂教学构念效果不佳。

Method: 提出"learning interactives"结构化框架，利用Gemini的生成式UI能力来构建有效的定制化交互模拟；通过教育工作者参与的初步研究，以及专家教师对多组模拟的教学评分进行评估。

Result: 通过教育工作者初步研究和专家教师对多样化模拟的教学评分两项评估进行检验（摘要未给出具体量化结果）。

Conclusion: 该框架有望以结构化方式借助生成式UI自动创建符合教学原则的交互模拟，弥补现成模型在教学精准度上的不足。

Abstract: It is well established that students learn best through active engagement, particularly via interactive experiences tied to real-world concepts. Creating such experiences, however, typically requires labor-intensive design, costly development, and continuous teacher guidance to achieve measurably effective outcomes. Recent advances in generative UI open the door for a future of practice where the creation of tailored interactive experiences is automated. However, off-the-shelf models lack optimization for pedagogical precision and learning principles, often yielding unsatisfactory results for complex pedagogical constructs. To address these limitations, we present learning interactives, a structured framework leveraging the generative UI capabilities of Gemini to construct effective customized interactive simulations. We evaluate our approach through both an initial study with educators as well as a pedagogical rating of a varied set of simulations by expert teachers.

</details>
